### [1] [Lunar laser ranging analyses of NGLR-1 data with PEP and prospects for MPAc: towards one millimeter range precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23393)
*G. Bargiacchi,J. Chandler,N. Burzillà,R. Campagnola,L. Porcelli,D. Currie,J. D. Carpenter,N. Boersma,L. Cacciapuoti,P. Firme,P. Ruzza,I. Lebouteiller,C. Courde,J. Chabé,J. Eckl,S. Merkowitz,N. Colmenares,R. Reasenberg,I. Shapiro,S. Dell'Agnello*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文分析了新一代月球激光测距反射器NGLR-1的观测数据，验证其将测距精度从厘米级提升至毫米级的能力，并展望其对广义相对论检验及月球地球物理学研究的推动作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史月面反射器（如阿波罗和月球车部署）受限于月球天平动引起的激光脉冲展宽，测距精度难以突破厘米量级；需发展新型大口径单体角反射器以消除脉冲色散、提升精度。

Method: 基于行星历表程序（PEP），分析NGLR-1（首个10 cm单体角反射器）在Apache Point、Wettzell和Grasse三地测站获取的原始测距数据、法向点（NPs）、残差及观测离散度。

Result: NGLR-1显著提升了飞行时间测量精度，优于阿波罗与月球车反射器阵列；同时表明ESA即将于2027年发射的MPAc（MoonLIGHT）反射器也具备良好应用前景。

Conclusion: NGLR-1的成功标志着月球激光测距迈入毫米级新阶段，为广义相对论与替代理论检验、月球内部结构与动力学研究提供更高精度观测基础。

Abstract: Since 1969, Lunar Laser Ranging (LLR) has provided critical data for investigating Earth-Moon dynamics, lunar internal structure, and tests of General Relativity (GR). However, the measurement precision of historical arrays (such as those from Apollo and Lunokhod missions) is inherently limited by laser pulse broadening induced by lunar librations. To overcome this limit, next-generation single Cube Corner Retroreflectors (CCRs) of large optical diameter have been developed to eliminate pulse dispersion, enabling LLR measurements to advance from centimeter-level precision down to the millimeter scale. In this work, we present a detailed analysis of LLR observations from NGLR-1 (Next Generation Lunar Retroreflector-1), the first single 10cm-diameter CCR deployed on the lunar surface. Carried out using the Planetary Ephemeris Program (PEP), this study investigates individual range measurements, normal points (NPs), residuals, and observational dispersion collected by the ground stations at Apache Point Observatory Lunar laser ranging station (APOL), Wettzell, and Grasse. The results: demonstrate the success of NGLR-1 in significantly enhancing time of flight measurement precision compared to legacy Apollo and Lunokhod retroreflector arrays; indicate similar good prospects also for ESA's MPAc laser retroreflector (MoonLIGHT with Pointing Actuators) to be launched in early 2027; pave the way for a new era of LLR observations and enhanced tests of GR, alternative gravity theories in general and improved lunar geophysics and geodesy.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文综述了当前高能X射线天文观测设施的现状与局限，强调在>10 keV能段缺乏聚焦能力是宽波段X射线天文学的主要瓶颈；提出亟需发展具备高角分辨率和高灵敏度的宽波段X射线任务，以支持多信使时代下对活动星系核、银河系致密天体等关键科学目标的研究，并指出技术进步已使探针级或旗舰级任务成为可行选项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软X射线设施丰富，但>10 keV能段聚焦能力稀缺，限制了热与非热过程的同步宽波段研究；多信使时代将涌现大量暂现源，亟需高分辨、高灵敏的宽波段X射线观测能力。

Method: 综述当前高能天文设施现状、关键科学需求（如宇宙X射线背景、银河系致密天体）及光学与探测器技术进展，并结合Astro2020十年调查目标，评估任务规模可行性。

Result: 确认宽波段X射线天文学已具备技术基础，可支撑低风险的探针级或旗舰级任务设计，实现数量级级的性能提升。

Conclusion: 发展高角分辨率、宽波段X射线任务是当前高能天体物理发展的紧迫需求，且技术上已成熟可行。

Abstract: Much of our understanding of high-energy astrophysical phenomena over the past decade has relied on coordinated observations extending from soft X-rays (<10 keV) to hard X-rays (>10 keV). While soft X-ray facilities remain abundant, focusing capabilities above ~10 keV are scarce, representing the primary gap for broad-band X-ray science. Simultaneous wide-band observations are essential to study thermal and non-thermal processes in extreme environments such as accretion discs, jets, and coronae. However, current high-energy observations remain limited by a lack of high angular resolution and sensitivity beyond Chandra's bandpass. With upcoming multi-messenger facilities set to dramatically increase variable and transient sources, a high angular resolution broad-band X-ray mission is critically needed.
  This report reviews the current landscape of high-energy facilities, outlines key science drivers, focusing on extragalactic accretion (the cosmic X-ray background) and Galactic compact objects, and examines the underlying technology landscape, including optics and detectors. Broad-band X-ray astrophysics is primed to address key questions from the Astro2020 Decadal Survey. While past focusing efforts have been limited to the Small Explorer class, technological advances now enable low-risk Probe- or Flagship-class architectures capable of delivering order-of-magnitude improvements in capabilities.

</details>

*Samuel Solod,Yoonsoo Kim,Elias R. Most*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应网格加密方法需要大量问题特定调参，缺乏普适可靠的加密准则；受谱方法中振荡指示器启发，寻求更少依赖人工调参、更稳健的网格加密策略。

Method: 基于谱方法中的振荡指示器思想，设计一种新的5点模板网格加密准则，扩展传统3点Löhner方案，并集成至天体物理流体动力学代码AthenaK中。

Result: 该准则在牛顿力学、狭义相对论和广义相对论框架下的磁流体动力学多个基准算例中均表现出良好鲁棒性与适用性。

Conclusion: 所提5点 stencil 加密准则是一种低调参、高鲁棒、跨相对论框架通用的自适应网格加密方法，显著提升了多尺度MHD模拟的效率与可靠性。

Abstract: Adaptive mesh refinement is a widely adopted strategy for grid-based numerical simulation codes to mitigate the high computational cost of multi-length scale problems. A robust and reliable mesh refinement criterion with minimal requirement for problem-specific tuning is critical for efficient and accurate simulations. Motivated by the oscillation indicators used in the spectral methods literature, we develop a new mesh refinement criterion employing a 5-point stencil for multi-dimensional magnetohydrodynamics simulations, essentially extending the conventional 3-point Löhner scheme. We implement the criterion in the astrophysical fluid dynamics code AthenaK and test it across a variety of benchmark problems in Newtonian, special relativistic, and general relativistic magnetohydrodynamics, demonstrating its robustness and versatility.

</details>

Abstract: Redrock is a software package for spectroscopic classification and redshift fitting, designed to use the full information from spectroperfectionism outputs: flux-calibrated spectra, uncorrelated errors, and a per-spectrum resolution matrix encoding the non-Gaussian wavelength-dependent spectral line-spread function. Redrock was originally developed for the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI), but the Extended Baryon Oscillation Spectroscopic Survey (eBOSS) also used Redrock as their primary galaxy redshift fitter prior to DESI. This paper presents the algorithmic details of Redrock and its performance on DESI data. On spectra from DESI DR2, Redrock achieves a redshift recovery rate of approximately 98% for the Bright Galaxy Survey (BGS) and Luminous Red Galaxies (LRG), 95.9% for Emission Line Galaxies (ELG), and 92.6% for quasars (QSO), and it delivers unbiased stellar radial velocities with a precision of roughly 5 km/s.

</details>


### [5] [Computation of complex dust grain optical properties: Optimisation method by equivalent representation (OMER)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24556)
*M. -A. Carpine,N. Ysard,A. Maury*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为OMER的优化方法，通过用等效均匀球体替代复杂单体，显著提升离散偶极子近似（DDA）在计算大型不规则/非均匀尘埃颗粒光学性质时的效率与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数值方法计算不规则或非均匀尘埃颗粒光学性质时计算资源消耗大，难以满足实际天体物理需求。

Method: 提出优化方法由等效表示（OMER），利用已知单体光学性质优化得到一个等效均匀球体，用于替代复杂单体参与大型聚集体的DDA计算。

Result: OMER在可见光至亚毫米波段比现有方法更准确，计算时间加速可达两个数量级以上。

Conclusion: OMER是一种快速且高精度的近似方法，适用于具有复杂形状、核-幔结构或层级结构的大尺寸尘埃聚集体的光学截面高效计算。

Abstract: Computing the optical properties of dust grains is important for understanding various astrophysical environments. However, the numerical methods used to compute these properties require significant computing resources for irregularly shaped or inhomogeneous particles that are representative of realistic grown dust. This study aims to provide an efficient yet accurate Discrete dipole approximation (DDA) computation method for large aggregates with 'complex' monomers and structural details or compositional inhomogeneities. We propose the optimisation method by equivalent representation (OMER) method, which uses optimisation of the optical properties of a known monomer to find an equivalent homogeneous sphere. This sphere can then be used as a substitute for the complex monomer in large aggregates. The method provides a good approximation of complex monomers, as demonstrated in test cases involving large aggregates. Compared to existing methods, OMER shows greater accuracy, particularly in the range from visible to submillimetre wavelengths. The gain in computing time depends on the grain but can exceed two orders of magnitude. The proposed method provides a fast and accurate approximation for estimating the optical properties of materials with complex shapes and paves the way for efficient computation cross-sections for complex dust grain aggregates with thin core-mantle structures or hierarchical aggregates of large sizes.

</details>

### [6] [An energy-dependent rescaling of the neutrino propagation Hamiltonian: Standard Model Extension identification, matter sector, and long-baseline degeneracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23343)
*Neda Razzaghi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了中微子传播哈密顿量乘以一个实能量依赖标量f(E) = 1 - η₀(E/E₀)² 的后果，该形式对应标准模型扩展（SME）中一个边际的CPT偶洛伦兹破缺项；真空部分可被重参数化消除，而物质势缩放项为CPT奇、维度五；仅p=2给出边际真空算符；数值估计显示其洛伦兹破缺系数与当前大气中微子实验限制相当；在恒定密度下，该效应等价于基线长度L按f(E)缩放，主要可观测量是Δm²₃₁的重新标度；拟合表明其可吸收部分振荡概率差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索中微子振荡中由能量依赖哈密顿量缩放引发的洛伦兹破缺效应，特别是其在标准模型扩展（SME）中的算符维度、CPT性质及可观测性，以检验量子引力可能带来的基本对称性破缺。

Method: 构建最小形式的能量依赖哈密顿量缩放H_η(E) = f(E) H₀(E)，其中f(E) = 1 − η₀(E/E₀)^p；分析其在真空和物质中的洛伦兹破缺SME系数维度与CPT性质；推导其对振荡概率、混合角、CP相位及质量劈裂Δm²₃₁的影响；在恒定密度近似下定量评估其对概率差的拟合能力。

Result: 发现仅p=2给出边际（dimension-4）CPT偶真空项，其SME系数由质量矩阵决定并可重参数化消除；物质项为CPT奇、dimension-5；对η₀=0.02、E₀=2.5 GeV，|ℒ₃₃|≈4×10⁻²⁴，接近当前大气中微子约束；在1297 km基线下，仅重拟合Δm²₃₁可吸收23%的概率差平方，四参数拟合可吸收41.5%。

Conclusion: 该能量依赖哈密顿量缩放模型虽引入洛伦兹破缺，但其真空部分不产生新物理效应（可重参数化），主要可观测效应体现为有效质量劈裂Δm²₃₁的能谱依赖缩放；其与实验数据存在显著简并，提示需结合多距离、宽能谱实验以打破参数退化、提升对洛伦兹破缺的检验灵敏度。

Abstract: We study the consequences of multiplying the neutrino propagation Hamiltonian by a real, energy-dependent scalar, $H_η(E)=f(E)H_0(E)$ with $f(E)=1-η_0(E/E_0)^2$, a minimal-length ansatz. Its vacuum part is a dimension-four, CPT-even Lorentz-violating term of the Standard Model Extension with coefficient $(\cL)_{ii}=-η_0(m_i^2-m_1^2)/(2E_0^2)$, diagonal in the mass basis and fixed by the mass matrix, hence removable by a reparametrization. The matter-potential rescaling has dimension-five behaviour and is CPT-odd. For $f=1-η_0(E/E_0)^p$ the vacuum term behaves as a dimension $p+2$ operator and the matter term as $p+3$, so only $p=2$ gives a marginal vacuum operator; $p=0$ is absorbed into the measured mass splittings, and non-integer $p$ admits no local operator. For $η_0=0.02$ at $E_0=2.5$~GeV we find $|(\cL)_{33}|\simeq4\times10^{-24}$, comparable to present atmospheric bounds, whereas a Planck-suppressed deformation with $β_0=\mathcal{O}(1)$ gives $3.2\times10^{-58}$. At constant density it is equivalent to $L\to f(E)L$: mixing angles and the Dirac phase are unchanged, the CP asymmetry is reparametrized rather than generated, and the leading observable is a rescaling of $\dm_{31}$. At $1297$~km a re-fit of $\dm_{31}$ alone absorbs $23$~per cent of the squared probability difference, and a four-parameter re-fit absorbs $41.5$~per cent. No statistical analysis is performed; degeneracy means a distance in probability space, not a confidence interval.

</details>


### [7] [Steerability in top quark decays at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23368)
*Tao Han,Navin McGinnis,Sepehr Salamat,Shufang Su,Xingzhou Yu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在大型强子对撞机（LHC）上利用顶夸克对产生过程探测量子EPR导引（steerability）的可行性，通过衰变方法和运动学方法分析了当前LHC及高亮度LHC（HL-LHC）的灵敏度，并发现 boosted区域和近阈值区域均具备显著统计显著性（最高达7.7σ），证实顶夸克对可呈现可证伪的量子导引行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EPR导引处于量子关联层级中 entanglement 和 Bell 非局域性之间，是更严格、更具应用价值的量子资源；在高能对撞机中探测其存在，可拓展量子信息在粒子物理中的实验前沿。

Method: 采用衰变方法（基于顶夸克衰变产物的自旋关联）与运动学方法（基于末态粒子动量分布构建可观测量），结合蒙特卡洛模拟与统计分析，在LHC和HL-LHC条件下评估 steerability 的可观测性。

Result: 在139 fb⁻¹数据下，boosted区域中半轻子道/双轻子道衰变法分别达到7.7σ/1.8σ显著性，steerability积分精度达1.5%/5.2%；运动学方法精度达0.05%；HL-LHC下衰变法精度提升至0.3%/1.1%；近阈值优化选样（M_tt ≲ 360–400 GeV）可达7σ/3.4σ。

Conclusion: 顶夸克对在LHC上展现出可实验证实的EPR导引特性，该测量鲁棒性强，不易受虚构量子态干扰，标志着高能对撞机成为检验中间层级量子关联的新平台。

Abstract: Quantum information observables in top quark pair production have opened a new experimental window on non-classical correlations of particle interactions at colliders. Einstein-Podolsky-Rosen (EPR) steering occupies a distinguished place in the quantum hierarchy, lying between entanglement and Bell nonlocality. We present a detailed collider analysis of steerability for top quark pairs at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC). In particular, we utilize both the decay and kinematic methods to estimate the expected sensitivity of the LHC and High Luminosity LHC (HL-LHC) to detect steerable states. We find that with the current LHC dataset of $139~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ the steerability of the $t\bar t$ state in the boosted region can be established at $7.7σ$ ($1.8σ$) in the semileptonic (dileptonic) channel with the decay method, corresponding to a measurement of the steerability integral at a precision of $1.5\%$ ($5.2\%$), while the kinematic method reaches a precision of $0.05\%$ in both channels. At the HL-LHC the decay-method precision improves to $0.3\%$ ($1.1\%$). Near threshold, where the inclusive selections used for entanglement and discord yield a state at the steerability boundary, an optimized selection $M_{t\bar{t}}\lesssim360$--$400$~GeV isolates a steerable singlet-dominated state, where the sensitivity is projected to reach $7σ$ ($3.4σ$). We also provide a detailed discussion on the interpretation of these results with respect to fictitious quantum states, and argue for the robust nature of the steerability measurement at colliders.

</details>

*Ariel Zhitnitsky*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出轴子夸克团（AQN）暗物质模型可统一解释不明异常现象（UAP）及其相关谜题，如超高加速度、无声超音速运动、无残留物等，并将其与球状闪电、天震等历史神秘现象联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释可见物质与暗物质能量密度相近（Ω_DM ∼ Ω_visible）这一普适性观测事实，且该模型最初并非为UAP设计，现被外推用于解释UAP现象。

Abstract: The Unidentified Anomalous Phenomena (UAP) events have been recorded for decades. However, the nature of these events remains unknown. We advocate an idea that the dark matter (DM) in form of the axion quark nuggets (AQN) made of standard model quarks (or {\it antiquarks}) and gluons could {\it simultaneously} explain many observed puzzling features which always accompany the UAP events, such as high velocity, enormous acceleration, absence of any remaining materials in affected area, etc. This long list of mysterious features also includes the absence of any sound which normally accompanies any supersonic motion. The AQN framework answers this last puzzle as well, which represents the title of the present work. The AQN dark matter model was invented long ago without any intension to apply the model to UAP events. Rather, it was invented with a single motivation to explain the observed similarity $Ω_{\rm DM}\sim Ω_{\rm visible}$ between visible and DM components, which represents a very generic feature of this framework, not sensitive to any parameters of the construction. In this work we use the same framework with the same set of parameters to study the observed UAP events. Essentially we argue that a specific class of UAP represents the close cousin of other mysteries known for decades (even centuries), such as sky-quakes, Ball Lightnings, and Mysterious Transient events. In other words, the UAPs represent different manifestations of exactly the same objects, the AQNs propagating in atmosphere. The fuel powering all these mysterious energetic events is one and the same: it is the antiquark core of the AQNs. We also present some suggestions on type of instruments required to study this specific (and well defined) type of the UAP events.

Abstract: Grand unification and string theory point to a quantum chromodynamics (QCD) axion with mass between $10^{-11}$ and $10^{-8}$ eV, while heterotic string theory suggests a mass of $0.5-0.8$ neV. We study which laboratory experiment could discover or exclude DFSZ-strength QCD axion dark matter at and above 0.5 neV at the lowest cost, which we estimate from scaling laws calibrated to historical data. We find that a lumped-element detector, building on the ABRACADABRA concept, can do so with demonstrated readout technology; an example is a shielded toroid about 14 m across with a 6 T NbTi magnet, whose magnet and cryostat we estimate at around 120 million Swiss francs (MCHF). If projected readout targets are met, a 5 m toroid at 25 MCHF suffices. We find these designs by optimizing the toroid, solenoid, and core lumped-element topologies under a common cost metric. The toroid emerges as the most promising and cost-effective topology. The optimal field of the toroid detector is far below the 16 T of the proposed DMRadio-GUT design, which would cost nearly twice as much as our optimum. Heterodyne searches with superconducting radio-frequency cavities, with cavity shapes that we optimize for price, cost between about the same as and twice as much as the lumped-element detectors, but require cavities of volumes $\sim50-2000$ m$^3$. We find that nuclear-magnetic-resonance searches (CASPEr) need orders-of-magnitude gains in spin polarization and coherence. We release axionMultiPhysics, an open-source finite-element and multiphysics package for, e.g., magnetoquasistatic and full-wave electromagnetics, superconductors, readout circuits, and mechanics, from which most of our numerical results were obtained.

</details>

Abstract: Using available information on proton tensor charges and constraints from perturbative quantum chromodynamics (QCD), Ansaetze are introduced for hadron-scale in-proton valence-quark transversity distributions functions (DFs). Abstract: Predicting the total angular momentum $J$ for hadronic resonances whose spins have not yet been experimentally determined constitutes an important step in the spectroscopy of observed hadrons. In the present study, we develop a data-driven model in order to predict the value of $ J $ for hadrons with unknown spins using a dataset of known mesons and baryons from the PDG, including information on their quark content, isospin, parity, mass and other spectroscopic features. In particular, we employ XGBoost classifiers along with nested optimization in order to discover spectroscopic patterns across different hadronic sectors. We also prepare a systematic list of hadrons with unknown or poorly determined values of $ J $, and study spin-parity assignments for those hadrons. Since the parity hypothesis is taken as an input to the model, our model predicts the value of $ J $ conditional on a given parity hypothesis rather than independently determining the full $J^P$ assignment. Our predictions for several theoretically studied states show good agreement with results from quark models, QCD sum rules, molecular models, and other phenomenological approaches, while predictions for some other states differ from existing theoretical assignments. Electric charge neutrality and beta equilibrium are imposed in order to describe matter under realistic neutron-star conditions, while nonlocality is implemented through a Gaussian form factor. We further show that charge neutrality and beta equilibrium modify the de Haas--van Alphen oscillations in the quark density, shifting the Landau-level thresholds for the $u$ and $d$ quarks in opposite directions relative to the isospin-symmetric case. The effect on the chiral transition is small ($\sim 5$MeV) for fields $B\lesssim 10^{19}$G, relevant for magnetar interiors, and becomes sizable only for $B\gtrsim 3\times10^{19}$G. The vertex correction brings the evolved charge weighted distribution $\sum{e^q E^q(ξ, x, t)}$ closer to the phenomenological parametrization. The diquark--antidiquark interaction quasipotential includes both spin--independent and spin--dependent relativistic contributions. Abstract: The sequential breaking of discrete flavor symmetries provides a theoretical framework that can explain quark mass and mixing angle hierarchies. Each model is identified by its field content, charge assignments and symmetry breaking pattern, leading to an enormous space of possible models; this motivates the investigation of computational approaches for identifying promising candidates. In this work, we focus on quark flavor models with the flavor symmetry group $G_F=(\mathbb{Z}_2)^p$, where $p$ is a positive integer; operators involving flavon fields which spontaneously break the flavor symmetry determine the size of the Yukawa matrix entries. We consider two search strategies: an active learning search employing a random forest regressor and a genetic search. The generation of candidate models is automated and models are evaluated using a loss function that takes into account the logarithmic discrepancy between the model prediction and target observable values as well as penalties for model complexity. We find that both our active learning and genetic algorithms significantly outperform random model generation, which we use as a control; our active learning approach identifies lower-loss models faster (i.e., with fewer exact model evaluations) than our genetic search, though each ultimately finds successful models. Our results support the effectiveness of active learning and evolutionary optimization as approaches to automate the search for promising models based on discrete flavor symmetries.

Abstract: We present a new class of 4-dimensional Grand Unified Theories where the gauge sectors of the standard model have a novel embedding in the unified group that does not factor through an $SU(5)$ subgroup. Abstract: We present the first spatially resolved study of polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbon (PAH) evolution in the galactic superwind of the nearby (d~3.5Mpc) galaxy NGC 253. In the southwest (SW) and northeast (NE) streamers, PAH intensity declines faster than $r^{-2}$, suggesting either PAH destruction or a decrease in gas column density or dust-to-gas ratio rather than a response to declining illumination alone. Band ratios trace changes in PAH properties, with the 11.3/7.7um ratio increasing with distance in the SW and northwest (NW) streamers and 3.3/7.7um and 3.3/11.3um decreasing in the SW and NE streamers, indicating larger, more neutral PAHs farther from the starburst. Given the thermal and dynamical properties of the gas in the SW streamer, cool gas is expected to survive acceleration in the outflow and enable PAH shielding. Compared with those of the prototypical starburst M82, the NGC 253 band ratios indicate faster evolution in PAH size. Thus, the NGC 253 wind may represent an earlier stage of outflow evolution in which small PAHs are destroyed near the starburst within the first few Myr.

Abstract: Rest-frame ultraviolet (UV) emission lines in galaxies provide important probes of the ionizing radiation, chemical abundances, and interstellar medium conditions. We compute Cloudy photoionization models using BPASS and BC03 stellar populations to test how lower C/O and higher gas density affect the UV diagnostics. We find that reducing C/O and increasing the gas density shift the star-forming grids into regions conventionally classified as composite or AGN-like. We therefore conclude that positions within these regions alone do not establish the presence of an AGN in the galaxies studied here. However, some lines remain enigmatic, including strong $\mathrm{He\,II}$, which is not reproduced by the updated models. These results highlight the need for UV diagnostic boundaries that account for the abundance patterns and gas conditions of galaxies during the epoch of reionization.

Abstract: Galaxy formation models necessitate that actively growing supermassive black holes, or quasars, strongly impact their environments. Theory suggests that quasar winds produce very hot gas bubbles that extend through the host galaxy and into the circumgalactic medium. Because this gas component is so hot and diffuse, the only way to measure its total energy is through the thermal Sunyaev-Zel'dovich (tSZ) effect. Here we report the discovery of a dual quasar system at z=2.37 which is embedded in a bubble of hot gas as detected (at 6 $σ$) by a tSZ flux decrement at millimeter wavelengths. The total thermal energy of the hot gas is 9.7 x 10$^{60}$ erg. This energy exceeds the amount expected from all of the hot gas in a 10$^{13}$ M$_\odot$ dark matter halo by a factor of 3.6, and constitutes at least 0.6% of the quasar's lifetime energy output. This considerable excess energy is more than sufficient to shut off star formation and heat the intergalactic medium, implying a significant impact from quasar-driven winds on their surroundings. Such feedback plausibly explains the steep decline of the bright end of the luminosity function of galaxies across the observable Universe, and thus provides evidence to further support leading galaxy evolution theory.

Abstract: Spectra from different surveys carry different systematics and are not calibrated against one another. Multi-survey foundation models for stellar spectra are then built by aligning embeddings of the same star observed across surveys. However, this does not ensure that the stellar physics is encoded in a form that a simple read-out can recover. Holding the cross-survey architecture fixed and varying only the alignment loss, we measure what an objective encodes from the principal-component geometry of the Gaia BP/RP (XP) spectra and from a linear probe of the stellar parameters. In a properly aligned embedding, the leading two principal components form a plane that recovers an analog of the Kiel diagram, the third component tracks [Fe/H], and [alpha/Fe] varies coherently across the plane, such that the chemically defined Galactic disks and accreted populations occupy distinct loci. We show that legibility, the ease of reading the information out, is highest for a training objective that never pushes stars apart, and instead constrains only the overall distribution of each survey's embeddings (JEPA). JEPA also exceeds the contrastive alternative at the same architecture, and the performance improves further as more surveys (Gaia XP+LAMOST LRS+DESI+APOGEE) are included. Predicted abundances for 8.0 million XP giants reproduce the main [alpha/Fe]-[Fe/H] structure of the Milky Way. These results indicate that the choice of alignment objective, not alignment alone, determines whether stellar labels are linearly recoverable.

Abstract: Constraining the properties of OB-star populations and ionized gas at low metallicity is essential for understanding a broad range of astrophysical objects, from stellar black holes to the first galaxies. However, such spectra are scarce at $Z\lesssim0.1Z\,\odot$, and candidate Very Massive Stars (VMS) remain elusive.

Some galaxies exhibit stellar wind features (group 1), while others do not (group 2). For group 1, we compare STARBURST99, GALAXEV, and BPASS predictions with observed stellar lines to constrain OB-population ages and masses. The inferred ages are too old for VMS to be present. We also use pySTARBURST99 and GALAXEV photoionization models to investigate the ionized gas in a subsample, finding no evidence for VMS-dominated ionization.

Moreover, reproducing the extreme optical and UV line ratios simultaneously requires unrealistic N/O, ionization parameters, or Lyman continuum escape fractions, suggesting that multiple ionizing-source populations may be needed.

Abstract: We investigate the role of the cosmic web on the origin and strength of lopsidedness in the distributions of luminous dwarf satellites around massive galaxies analogs in the high-resolution hydrodynamic simulation NewHorizon. Using common lopsidedness estimators, we find that $>25\%$ of our sample display ``statistically significant" lopsidedness incompatible with expectations for dark matter sub-haloes. The NewHorizon sample is generally skewed towards being more lopsided than naive expectations. This cautions against using dark matter or under-resolved hydrodynamic simulations as a null-hypothesis to compare to real systems. We find broad, hemispheric lopsidedness to be much more prevalent than narrow lopsidedness inherited from group infall. Across all systems, the dividing plane between the richest and emptiest hemispheres is systematically aligned with a nearby intermediate-scale cosmic filament. Further analysis reveals that the lopsidedness of systems is largely inherited from that of the network of filaments feeding them. The most populated side almost systematically contains the highest number of filaments. Such anisotropies of local filament networks are often indicative of on-going or recent zippers (filament merger $<2$ Gyr). Converging filamentary structures therefore funnel satellites preferentially onto one side of host halos, naturally generating lopsided configurations without requiring galaxy mergers. Twister remnants (of high orbital momentum mergers of filaments) are also found on the richest hemisphere of our systems, perhaps reminiscent of vorticity preventing mergers hence causing satellites' pooling. These results suggest that lopsidedness is primarily an outcome of the evolution of the intermediate-scale cosmic web. This highlights the need for increased hydrodynamic resolution in simulations.

</details>

Method: 通过JWST的NIRCam和MIRI仪器开展三个相隔3–5年的多历元成像观测，总时长约420小时，实现比Roman WFI更高分辨率（3倍）、更高测光与天体测量精度及更广的窄带与中红外光谱覆盖。

Abstract: The center of the Milky Way is the densest and most extreme environment in our Galaxy, its most active star forming region, and the only galaxy nucleus where we can resolve stars down to about a solar mass on the main sequence. The Galactic Center (GC) contains a nuclear stellar disk, a nuclear star cluster, and a supermassive black hole, all embedded within the so-called Central Molecular Zone, which showcases conditions of the interstellar medium that are typically found in high-redshift star-forming regions. The GC hosts the highest concentration of massive stars in the Milky Way and hosts a large concentration of rare objects, such as Wolf Rayet stars and X-ray binaries. The GC is therefore an astrophysical target of outstanding importance. A multi-epoch JWST program enables novel science including the lifetime of planet-forming disks, the search for intermediate mass black holes, direct measurements of the multi-scale gravitational potential, and a deep measurement of the star formation history. Multi-epoch imaging observations with JWST's NIRCam and MIRI instruments will go far beyond Roman WFI surveys in terms of depth and astrometric and photometric precision because of its $3\times$ better resolution, and it expands spectral coverage in narrow bands and mid-infrared wavelength coverage. The proposed observations would correspond to a Treasury Program of about 420\,h, distributed over three epochs spaced three to five years apart.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确描绘CNOPS元素的宇宙起源，建立能同时拟合本地与河外星系观测约束的自洽银河系化学演化框架。

Method: 整合文献丰度数据，构建包含单星/双星核合成产额、恒星自转及新星核合成的GCE模型，并联合优化CNOPS多元素丰度约束。

Abstract: As fundamental bio-essential elements, carbon, nitrogen, oxygen, phosphorus, and sulphur (CNOPS) warrant a precise mapping of their cosmic origins. In this paper, we present the SPONGE project (Sulphur, Phosphorus, Oxygen, Nitrogen, and carbon Galactic Evolution), aimed at establishing a self-consistent galactic chemical evolution (GCE) framework capable of simultaneously reproducing local abundance benchmarks along with key observational constraints in external galaxies. Here, we focus on the Milky Way. We compile literature data and construct GCE models incorporating various single- and binary-star yields, taking stellar rotation and nova nucleosynthesis into account. Fast rotators are confirmed to be essential for primary 14N and 13C production at low metallicities. Although our homogeneous models do not reach the lowest 12C/13C ratios observed in some halo stars, this discrepancy should not be taken as a refutation of the model, as observational selection effects may apply. Predicted C and N isotopic ratios along the disc agree remarkably well with those inferred from radio observations of star-forming regions. Notably, our models explain Galactic P enrichment as originating predominantly from massive stars, without any ad hoc adjustment of the yields or the need for a significant nova contribution. Conversely, novae are required to synthesize non-negligible fractions of 13C and 15N. Optimizing GCE trajectories against a comprehensive CNOPS ensemble simultaneously, rather than single diagnostics one at a time, establishes a robust baseline for future work. Remaining open questions, such as the predicted overproduction of 12C at low metallicities and 34S along the disc, alongside the abundance scatter of some key elements, provide concrete targets for subsequent papers in this series.

</details>

Abstract: Gaia Science Alerts provide an all-sky stream of photometric transients, including events consistent with gravitational microlensing. For such events, forthcoming epoch astrometry from the Gaia mission offers the possibility of testing photometric lens mass predictions. While microlensing parallax inferred from the photometric light curve can constrain the event geometry, a direct lens mass measurement additionally requires the angular Einstein radius, which may be accessible through astrometric microlensing. We aim to construct a pre-Data-Release-4 (DR4) sample of Gaia microlensing events for which the available photometry provides a measurable microlensing-parallax signal. This allows us to derive statistical predictions for the lens mass and distance. We forward-modelled these events to predict the detectability of the astrometric microlensing signal in Gaia DR4. We identify 56 Gaia Science Alerts microlensing events with 71 point-source point-lens solutions with annual parallaxes that satisfy our fit-quality, posterior-boundedness, and parallax-significance criteria. Lens masses and distances were inferred using two prior-based frameworks, pyLIMASS and the Besançon Galaxy Model, which combine the measured microlensing parameters and Gaia source information with stellar-population and Galactic models. These inferred parameters were used to simulate Gaia DR4-like along-scan astrometry and estimate the detectability of the astrometric microlensing signal. The inferred lens masses and distances remain strongly prior-dependent without a direct angular Einstein radius measurement. Among the 284 event-solution-prior branches, 47 are classified as high-confidence astrometric detections using Gaia DR4-like simulations. These predictions can be tested once epoch astrometry is released in Gaia DR4.

Abstract: The interstellar medium (ISM) of the Small Magellanic Cloud (SMC) provides a unique laboratory for studying magnetised gas in a low-metallicity, tidally disturbed environment. This is consistent with a turbulent cascade from large-scale structures. Analysed through RM synthesis and Stokes $QU$-fitting, we attribute the depolarisation to a turbulent magneto-ionic medium within the telescope's beam ($\lesssim6~\mathrm{pc}$). Furthermore, as previously identified in the Large Magellanic Cloud, depolarisation in the SMC increases with emission measure; however, the underlying Faraday dispersion is $\sim$6 times weaker. With the thermal electron density smooth on the scales of the field fluctuations, this depolarisation is likely driven by magnetic field fluctuations.

Abstract: We present LEMUR, a publicly accessible archive of uniformly reduced, deep Chandra images of 2,569 galaxies and galaxy clusters (including groups), comprising 5,505 ACIS observations and 138.8 Ms of exposure. By combining the publicly available imaging observations of each target, we provide background-subtracted, exposure-corrected images in broad, soft, and hard bands at the native ACIS pixel scale of 0.492 arcsec. Our primary goal is to make the wealth of archival Chandra observations accessible to researchers without requiring specialized expertise in X-ray data reduction.
  Each observation is reprocessed with CIAO, filtered for background flares, and reduced with settings chosen to preserve faint diffuse emission; the merged images are exposure corrected and background subtracted using the ACIS blank-sky backgrounds in a broad (0.5-7.0 keV), a soft (0.5-2.0 keV), and a hard (2.0-7.0 keV) band. For every object, we measure an X-ray center at the peak of its diffuse emission, and we provide a morphological similarity search based on embeddings from the AstroCLIP foundation model, in which nearest neighbors share cool-core status in 73% of matched cluster pairs, compared with 51% by chance.
  Photometry of 275 point sources agrees with the Chandra Source Catalog to within 3% in the broad band. The images, along with their counts images, background models, exposure maps, and quick-look stamps, are available through a searchable web archive at https://lemur-archive.lemur-xray.workers.dev/. The catalog tables are archived on Zenodo (doi:10.5281/zenodo.24351915), and all code is open source at https://github.com/crhea93/Lemur.

Abstract: JWST has uncovered compact high-redshift sources with red, $\sim 5000$ K optical continua and broad Balmer lines, known as "little red dots" (LRDs). Whether these lines trace gas orbiting a massive black hole or electron scattering in a dense, Eddington-limited envelope around a lighter black hole leaves inferred masses spanning $10^{4}$--$10^{8}\,M_\odot$. We test both pictures by uniformly refitting public JWST/NIRSpec spectra of 29 LRDs, 15 other JWST broad-line sources, and luminosity-matched SDSS quasars. At fixed $\mathrm{H}α$ luminosity, LRD broad $\mathrm{H}α$ widths are remarkably uniform: their intrinsic scatter is 0.054 dex ($<0.075$ dex at 95%), less than half that of quasars (0.12 dex), clustering near $1760\ \mathrm{km\,s^{-1}}$ outside the three brightest sources. Because single-epoch virial masses scale as width squared, they scatter intrinsically by $<0.16$ dex at fixed $\mathrm{H}α$ luminosity, making virial masses and mass functions nearly deterministic restatements of luminosity regardless of the underlying physics. Compared with the Keplerian speed $v_{\rm K}$ at the photosphere of an Eddington-limited envelope, computed from each source's measured continuum luminosity and temperature, observed widths are $2.5$--$3.0\,v_{\rm K}$ ($2\text{--}3\times$ standard expectations for virial factors $f_{\rm vir}\approx0.5\text{--}1$), with a quarter of the flux above escape speed and no detected continuum-luminosity dependence at fixed $\mathrm{H}α$ luminosity. Bound orbits outside a spherical photosphere thus require sub-Eddington luminosities ($\leq 0.10\text{--}0.30\,L_{\rm Edd}$), whereas an Eddington or super-Eddington envelope requires uniform $2\text{--}3\times$ non-gravitational broadening within the $\mathrm{H}α$-emitting gas.

The $^{12}$CO(2-1) disk exhibits a nearly face-on, ring-like morphology with regular disk rotation and a steep central velocity rise, providing clean dynamical constraints on the central gravitational potential. Our best-fit model yields $M_{\rm BH} = 2.40^{+0.52}_{-0.32}[{\rm stat.}] \pm 0.91 [{\rm sys.}] \times 10^9~M_\odot$ and a stellar mass-to-light ratio of $M/L_{\rm F814W} = 4.11^{+0.99}_{-0.27}[{\rm stat.}] \pm 0.17 [{\rm sys.}]~M_\odot/L_\odot$ (both at $1σ$ confidence level). The required dust masses are small relative to global reservoirs, so dust scattering remains feasible in individual regions. Comparing stellar-population ages and jet-propagation times, we identify seven UV-emitting regions as strong candidates for recently initiated jet-triggered star formation. Together, these alignments reveal an assembly-linked direction along which jets may shock or compress material and hidden-quasar radiation may illuminate it.

With molecular H$_2$, CH$_3$SH also forms efficiently. The results support an alternative pathway in which C reacts with H$_2$ to form CH$_2$, which abstracts H from H$_2$S. The resulting CH$_3$ and SH radicals can then recombine to form CH$_3$SH. The infrared bands and TPD fragmentation patterns support the formation of H$_2$CS and CH$_3$SH in the ice at 10 K. These findings expand the set of viable solid-state pathways to sulfur-bearing molecules and constrain models of sulfur chemistry in prestellar environments.

Abstract: An important challenge in cosmology is to describe the formation of galaxies and structures, starting from small inhomogeneities in the early Universe, which later grew and formed the so- called dark matter halos. We consider the dark matter to be composed of ultralight bosons and simplify the general complex problem of halo formation by using the time-dependent variational analysis approach. This yields, for the first time, a fully analytical equation for the time-evolution of perturbations towards isolated haloes. We follow the evolution of the halo size and density during different stages of its formation, revealing the role of the background density, Universe expansion, and quantum pressure.

Abstract: High-quality observations reveal that internal dynamics of axisymmetric ETGs can be more complicated than commonly adopted in modelizations based on the Jeans equations (JEs), with the identity of the radial and vertical velocity dispersions. A well known generalization is obtained by solving the JEs with the prescription of a proportionality factor $b$ between the radial and vertical velocity dispersions; in its simplest form $b$ is assumed constant, while in recent studies $b=b(z)$. We investigate the properties of the $b$ parameter and of the other kinematical decomposition parameters commonly adopted in modeling in a sample of ETGs taken from the high-resolution cosmological simulations TNG50. From a selection of highly axisymmetric and passive TNG50 (satellite and central) galaxies at equilibrium, we recovered the maps of $b(R,z)$ and of other important kinematic quantities entering the JEs. In all the selected galaxies the only significantly non-zero velocity streaming component is the azimuthal one, and the velocity dispersion tensor is cylindrically aligned. The maps of $b$ are clearly not constant, and not stratified over planes, instead they present a common structure, with the largest values values near the center, and a wedge structure at large (cylindrical) radii. A phenomenological formula for $b(R,z)$ to be used in applications is proposed. Also the other decomposition parameters are non-constant. No significant differences are found as a function of galaxy flattening and mass, while some difference is observed between satellite and central galaxies. The internal dynamics of the TNG50 galaxies present interesting regularities, worth of investigation. On the other hand, in Jeans based studies of real galaxies some simple assumptions for the choice of anisotropy and kinematical decomposition parameters do not seem appropriate. [abridged]

Abstract: In this paper we present observations of the magnetic field of the starburst galaxy NGC 253 made in polarized 850 $μ$m dust emission using the POL-2 polarimeter on the James Clerk Maxwell Telescope (JCMT). While the magnetic field typically traces the galactic spiral structure, we see significant deviations in field direction associated with the presence of stellar and/or supernova feedback, with the mean plane-of-sky magnetic field direction offset 24$^{\circ}$ from the galactic plane. Polarization fraction is anticorrelated with total 850 $μ$m intensity, with a power-law index of 0.72$\pm$0.03. Using the Fasano-Franceschini test, we find that the positions of POL-2 detections and radio sources in the spiral arms are inconsistent with one another, indicative of depolarization resulting from magnetic fields being tangled on scales smaller than the 240 pc JCMT 850 $μ$m beam in sites of star formation and feedback. Moreover, in both the starburst superwind and in one star-forming clump in the spiral arms, we observe a magnetic field direction consistent with being directed out of the plane of the galaxy. We thus hypothesise that magnetic fields in the giant molecular clouds (GMCs) of NGC 253 are undergoing an evolutionary sequence from a relatively ordered field parallel to the spiral arms in the pre-star-formation gas, to a complex field caused by the interplay of turbulence, gravity and small-scale feedback in relatively young GMCs, to a field that is reordered out of the plane of the galaxy as large-scale HII region expansion reshapes the gas and dust of galactic spiral arms.

Conclusion: IC 342是极少数同时具备直接动力学测定IMBH与完整表征NSC的无核球旋涡星系之一，该结果为致密天体（IMBH与NSC）的共同演化模型提供了关键锚点。

Abstract: We present the first direct stellar-dynamical measurement of an intermediate-mass black hole (IMBH) in IC 342, a nearby nearly face-on ($i\approx31^\circ$) bulgeless spiral at $D=3.45\pm0.13$ Mpc, using near-infrared integral-field spectroscopy with Keck/OSIRIS LGS-AO. The heavy foreground extinction ($A_{\mathrm{F814W}}\approx0.74$ mag) renders the nucleus inaccessible to optical spectroscopy, making $K$-band observations at $\sim$2.3 $μ$m the only viable path to high-resolution nuclear kinematics. Two-dimensional stellar kinematics extracted from the CO bandheads via pPXF at $0.062''$ resolution reveal a central velocity dispersion rising from $\sim$42 km s$^{-1}$ at $r\gtrsim0.2''$ to a peak of $\sim$49 km s$^{-1}$, providing kinematic evidence for a central dark mass within the nuclear star cluster (NSC; $M_\mathrm{NSC}\approx1.17\times10^7~M_\odot$). Modeling these kinematics with an ensemble of four JAM models, spanning cylindrical and spherical velocity-ellipsoid alignments and two PSF representations, combined with an MGE mass model constructed from a three-Sérsic plus exponential-disk decomposition of archival HST/WFC3 F814W imaging and incorporating a physically motivated stellar mass-to-light ratio gradient, driven by the young central NSC population, yields $M_\mathrm{BH}=(1.95\pm0.61)\times10^5~M_\odot$. Accounting for this stellar population gradient is essential for the detection: standard constant-$M/L$ models systematically overestimate the central stellar mass, hiding the BH signature and yielding only an upper limit. The stellar orbits are near-isotropic ($β\approx0$), typical of fast-rotating late-type spirals. This measurement places IC 342 among the rare bulgeless spirals with a direct dynamical $M_{\rm BH}$ at the IMBH mass scale, where both the NSC and BH are directly characterized, providing a new anchor for compact massive object co-evolution models.

Abstract: Infrared arc-shaped structures around early-type stars are commonly interpreted as stellar wind bow shocks, although their morphology may also be shaped by gas motions and density variations in the surrounding interstellar medium. We present a multiwavelength study of the infrared arc associated with the nearby $α$ Crucis system, comprising HD 108248 (B0.5 IV), HD 108249 (B1 V), and HD 108250 (B4 V). We combine WISE, Herschel, Planck, stellar astrometry, and eROSITA X-ray data to investigate the structure and its relation to the stellar components. The WISE W4 ridge extends over $\sim$0.65 pc and has a median PSF-corrected width of $\sim$0.037 pc. The HD 108248/HD 108249 pair lies $\sim$0.13 pc from the ridge, compared with $\sim$0.17 pc for HD 108250. Relative to the mean motion of the Lower Centaurus-Crux (LCC) subgroup of the Scorpius-Centaurus association, the close pair is consistent with LCC, whereas HD 108250 has a transverse peculiar velocity of only $\sim$1.8 km s$^{-1}$. Its motion lies within $\sim$23$^\circ$ of the geometrical symmetry axis of the infrared arc, but points in the opposite sense from the arc. Herschel maps show slightly higher median dust temperature ($ΔT_d\sim$0.8 K) and molecular-hydrogen column density ($ΔN_{\rm H2}\sim$7$\times$10$^{20}$ cm$^{-2}$) toward the W4-defined region than in the nearby background. On larger scales, Planck maps reveal a strongly structured microwave environment around the system. Both the HD 108248/HD 108249 pair and HD 108250 are detected by eROSITA, with the former approximately 60 times more luminous in X-rays. The combined morphology, kinematics, and dust properties do not support an unambiguous classical high-velocity bow-shock interpretation. Instead, the arc likely traces a weak interaction between the multiple stellar system and an inhomogeneous ambient medium.

Gas-phase metallicities are derived from these ratios using strong-line calibrations recently derived from high-redshift galaxies. We find 912$^{+88}_{-80}$ kpc to be the best-fitting heliocentric distance to the satellite, consistent with M31's outer halo. With $M_{V} \sim -5.00^{+0.51}_{-0.44}$ and a half-light radius of $r_{\rm h} \sim 31^{+13}_{-9}$pc HEC14 lies in the size-luminosity gap between globular clusters and dwarf galaxies. We validate this methodology on a toy model and an isolated galaxy hydrodynamical simulation snapshot, demonstrating that the technique reliably distinguishes three viewing angle brackets: side-on (0° - 35°), intermediate (35° - 65°), and end-on (65° - 90°). Derived nuclear ring sizes and deprojected bar lengths are broadly consistent with measurements from more face-on literature samples. This method offers a practical and computationally inexpensive means of constraining bar properties in edge-on galaxies, complementing full dynamical modelling approaches.

We then test the model on EAGLE to measure its robustness after applying a simple domain-alignment step that corrects for a relative shift in the $M_*$ distribution between TNG100 and EAGLE. We further augment the training data by combining feature sets from neighbour samples with varying lower $M_*$ thresholds. On the TNG100 test set, the central/satellite classifier reaches purity and completeness $=0.97$ (0.95) in 3D (projected redshift) space at the threshold where purity=completeness, and the halo-mass regressor achieves $r^2$=0.98 (0.93) when evaluated on true centrals and $r^2=0.93$ (0.93) when evaluated on inferred centrals. Applied to EAGLE after alignment, the classifier achieves purity/completeness $=0.95$ (0.95), while halo-mass inference yields $r^2=0.91$ ($0.87$) for true centrals and $r^2=0.83$ (0.77) for inferred centrals; training-data augmentation most improves the halo-mass estimation task. Satellites misclassified as centrals underpredict the true masses of host halos, especially if they are closer to a lower mass halo's central than its own host's central and/or has a significantly lower $M_*$ than its own central. These results show that survey-accessible information such as projected separations and stellar masses - combined with simulation-trained models and domain alignment - can enable reliable central identification and host halo mass estimation to an $r^2\geq 0.85$.

Abstract: Context. Bars and their boxy/peanut (B/P) shapes drive the secular evolution of disc galaxies, redistributing angular momentum and reshaping the radial and vertical structure of stellar discs. Bars occur in bulgeless to bulge-dominated hosts. Bars are known to grow and slow down, but how this relates to host galaxy structure remains poorly understood. Aims. We investigate how the formation and evolution of bars and B/P structures depend on the initial stellar mass and bulge fraction of Milky Way (MW)-like galaxies, and explore the links between disc kinematic settling, bar formation, resonance structure, and vertical thickening. Methods. We analyse fifteen isolated MW-like hydrodynamical simulations run with AREPO for 3 Gyr, spanning three stellar masses and five bulge fractions, with live stellar, bulge, and dark matter components. We measure bar properties via Fourier decomposition, locate resonances from circular and epicyclic frequencies, and quantify B/P evolution from the vertical stellar distribution. Results. Bar formation timing depends strongly on galaxy structure. Low-mass galaxies generally form bars only after global disc kinematics have settled, whereas the most massive systems can form bars while the orbital distribution is still evolving. All bars slow down and grow in strength and size, and remain in the slow-bar regime. B/P structures form shortly after the bar and keep growing, appearing much earlier in more massive and disc-dominated galaxies. Only one model shows a clear buckling event; the rest form B/P bulges through smooth secular evolution. Conclusions. Initial stellar mass and bulge fraction strongly regulate the timing of bar and B/P formation. We find a coherent evolutionary sequence linking bar growth, bar slowdown, and B/P assembly, whose timing is set by the initial disc structure rather than by its global kinematic settling.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决格点场论中由于复玻尔兹曼权重导致的符号问题，传统方法难以直接采样，需寻找替代方案。

Method: 利用生成扩散模型（尤其是基于能量的扩散模型）从复朗之万动力学数据中学习未知的实半正定分布；该分布可作为标准马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛模拟的权重。

Abstract: Generative diffusion models offer a new route to ensemble generation in lattice field theory. The backward, or denoising, process underlying these models is a stochastic process with a learned, time-dependent drift, which leads to a clear connection with stochastic quantisation. After reviewing this correspondence, I turn to theories with a sign problem. There the complex Boltzmann weight obstructs direct sampling, but complex Langevin dynamics can sometimes evade the sign problem by generating configurations on a complexified manifold, distributed according to a real and semi-positive distribution. This distribution is not known in closed form. I discuss how this distribution can be learned from complex Langevin data using score-based and, in particular, energy-based diffusion models. The latter give direct access to the unnormalised distribution itself, which can subsequently be used as the weight in a standard Markov chain Monte Carlo simulation. Results are shown for a simple quartic model with a complex action.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒙特卡洛数值计算的关联函数常因统计涨落而违反由因果性导出的Hankel矩阵正定性约束，需一种能严格尊重其凸锥结构的修复方法。

Method: 基于因果性诱导的凸锥结构与统计协方差矩阵，设计约束投影算法，采用原始-对偶内点法求解；区别于以往仅利用凸性的工作，显式利用锥结构。

Result: 实现了对噪声关联函数的唯一分解（因果/非因果分量），并为因果bootstrap中的抹平谱密度提供了可观测量特异的频率学置信区间。

Abstract: As a consequence of causality, Euclidean-time correlation functions in quantum field theory are subject to constraints in the form of positivity conditions on certain Hankel matrices. Such conditions define convex conic structures. Correlation functions computed numerically using Monte Carlo techniques generally contain statistical fluctuations that violate the positivity conditions. This work describes a projection procedure, based on the convex conic geometry induced by the positivity conditions and a statistical covariance matrix, that can be used to restore positivity, providing a unique decomposition for noisy correlators into causal and acausal components. In contrast to previous literature, this work explicitly leverages the conic structure and not just the convex nature of the causal space. The projection can be computed using primal-dual interior-point methods. Constrained projection gives rise to observable-specific frequentist confidence intervals for smeared spectral densities in the causal bootstrap of [Phys. Rev. D 114, 074510 (2026)]. Analytic results are supported with numerical examples.

</details>

Result: 该方法能给出具有名义覆盖度的误差估计：对由数据主导的输出（如相关器、抹平谱密度、阶跃函数）覆盖良好；系统误差在高统计极限下不随统计量增加而减小；附加理论约束可显著降低系统误差且不损害覆盖度；对长程关联器，其误差与边界法一致。

Conclusion: 所提方法统一处理了正定性约束与统计不确定性，使系统误差具有清晰物理含义和可控性，适用于强子谱等需严格满足物理约束的格点QCD分析。

Abstract: Positivity of the spectral density, combined with a $χ^2$ measure of compatibility with the data, bounds every linear functional of the spectral density of a Euclidean correlator to an interval. We show how such an interval is turned into a central value with statistical and systematic errors that have a definite meaning. We first show that, at typical precision, the best positive fit of noisy data has $χ^2_{\min}>0$, with a probability bounded from below by the distance of the noise-free data from the boundary of the set of data of positive spectra. Numerically, it does not depend on the number of states. The threshold must therefore be set relative to $χ^2_{\min}$. With $Δχ^2=1$, the interval reproduces Gaussian error propagation when positivity does not act, and positivity can only narrow it. We define the statistical error by the jackknife and the systematic error as the remaining, non-Gaussian part of the interval, which becomes a systematic error that does not decrease with the statistics in the high-statistics limit. Coverage tests for correlators and for smeared spectral densities and step functions give nominal coverage for outputs determined by the data and mostly conservative coverage otherwise. The exceptions are effects of the statistical error on the error estimate that disappear as the statistics is increased. Additional bounds on the spectral density, for example from perturbation theory, further reduce the systematic error substantially without loss of coverage. For long-distance correlators the method uses the same information as the bounding method, and its error agrees with that of the bounding method up to the remaining gap between the bounds.

</details>

Moreover, our results are consistent with weak couplings and fermions can only impact the Higgs potential at NNLO. This implies a so far unidentified mechanism in an SU(2) scalar-fermion-gauge system that produces a non-zero vacuum expectation value. Abstract: The retarded Green's function of gravitational waves in a spatially flat Friedmann--Lemaître--Robertson--Walker universe contains, besides the light-cone term, a tail supported inside the light cone. In this sense, we fill the interior of the light cone. We solve this problem directly in position space for any expansion history, and compare exact solutions in $Λ$CDM independently with a convergent double series about the coincidence point, and with an approximate analytic interaction model. Our novel solutions allow us to follow the tail throughout the entire expansion history, from the electroweak epoch to today. Under a common, explicitly specified reference prescription for the axial clock, rotational frame, and electromagnetic potentials, the two branches also have identical thermodynamic readouts, including energy, temperature, angular velocity, and electric and magnetic potentials. Abstract: We develop a model, that we call local semiclassical gravity, based on the distributional semiclassical Einstein field equations (EFEs) sourced by the local quantum state, i.e. the reduced density matrix of the local system updated by collapses (only) in its past light cone. We propose this as the basis of a relativistically causal generally covariant gravitisation of relativistic quantum theory in $1+3$ spacetime dimensions. We derive the weak-field limit which exhibits the explicitly causal dynamical structure of the theory, and recover the Schrödinger-Newton (SN) system in the nonrelativistic limit. This proposal can thus be understood as a causal completion of the SN equation, a nonlinear extension to quantum theory. We analyze relativistic quantum measurements in this nonlinear setup, showing that these do not lead to superluminal signalling if quantum states update (only) in the future light-cone of the measurement region. We give explicit examples of quantum measurements on a spatial superposition and on entangled subsystems showing how, in the weak field limit, discontinuities in the expectation value of the stress-energy tensor can be consistent with distributional solutions to the (linearised) semiclassical EFEs. We also highlight that the theory allows non-standard measurements via its nonlinearities. These effective ``readout devices'' can approximately determine a quantum state without affecting it through the state's influence on the classical gravitational field and hence can implement approximate quantum state cloning. Nonetheless, the theory does not allow superluminal signalling, although it does violate the quantum no-signalling principle. We further show that the classical gravitational field is generally not locally causal in the sense of Bell. We present several paths to test the model against low-energy quantum gravity in tabletop experiments.

Abstract: Far from its core, a vortex in a superfluid is acoustically equivalent to a massless spinning cosmic string, and the scattering of phonons reduces to an Aharonov--Bohm (AB) problem. Beyond this limit, we compute the Lorentz and Poincaré holonomies in closed form, the Lorentz and Poincaré holonomies of the \emph{exact} acoustic geometry of an irrotational vortex for circular loops of arbitrary radius~$r$. Using the Painlevé--Gullstrand tetrad, which is regular inside the ergoregion, we show that the Lorentz holonomy changes conjugacy class at the ergosurface $r=\re=Γ/2πc$: it is a rotation by $2π\sqrt{1-\re^{2}/r^{2}}$ outside, a null rotation on, and a boost of rapidity $2π\sqrt{\re^{2}/r^{2}-1}$ inside. The invariant translational component of the Poincaré holonomy is a time shift $τ(r)=(Γ/c^{2})(1-v_s^{2}/c^{2})^{-1/2}$, which we identify with half the acoustic Sagnac delay measured in the proper time of a static observer; it reduces to the spinning-string value $Γ/c^{2}$ as $r\to\infty$. No torsion is required: the translational holonomy follows from the Cartan development of the Levi-Civita connection. The non-topological part of the geometry leaves a long-range imprint on phonon scattering: the centrifugal term is $m^{2}+2mα$ instead of the AB form $(m+α)^{2}$, with $α=ωΓ/2πc^{2}$, so the $s$ wave is not phase shifted. As a consequence the transverse cross section, which controls the Iordanskii force, is $qσ_{\perp}=2πα-π^{3}α^{3}/3+O(α^{5})$, rather than the AB value $\sin 2πα$. We show that the near-zone curvature and any short-range, parity-symmetric core physics enter $σ_\perp$ only at $O(α^{5})$, so the $α^{3}$ term is universal. We give estimates for superfluid $^4$He, atomic condensates, and shallow-water analogues.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate the scattering of scalar acoustic waves by the rigid core of a point defect described by the global-monopole geometry, \(dl^2=dr^2+α^2r^2dΩ^2\), allowing for both a deficit (\(α<1\)) and an excess (\(α>1\)) of solid angle. Because the metric is not asymptotically flat, a global plane wave cannot be defined, and we formulate the scattering problem in terms of partial-wave phase shifts. These separate exactly into a topological contribution, \(\fracπ{2}(\ell-s_\ell)\), and a core contribution determined by spherical Bessel functions of noninteger order \(s_\ell\). We derive a closed-form expression for the topological amplitude, supplemented by an absolutely convergent remainder. The amplitude is singular on the cone \(θ_0=π|1-1/α|\), corresponding to the universal deflection angle of acoustic geodesics. The core amplitude obeys a generalized Rayleigh law, with a dipole exponent \(\sqrt{8+α^2}/α\). In the absence of translational isometries, the radiation force must be evaluated directly on the core surface. We derive a partial-wave expression for this force and show that its leading low-frequency contribution is linear in the topological phase. For every \(α>1/\sqrt{6}\), except \(α=1\), the force is negative within a low-frequency interval \(ka<ka_c(α)\), pulling the core toward the source. Abstract: Gravitational-wave lensing in the wave-optics regime requires evaluating the Fresnel--Kirchhoff diffraction integral, whose integrand is highly oscillatory and must be evaluated numerically, as closed-form solutions exist only for a few lens models. We present a novel, efficient algorithm that splits the integration domain at a point beyond the region containing the lensed images. The bounded head region is integrated along the real axis with Gauss--Legendre quadrature, while the semi-infinite tail is rotated into the complex plane, where the integrand decays exponentially, and is evaluated with Gauss--Laguerre quadrature. Since the image region is never deformed, the method avoids the treatment of poles and branch cuts required by existing contour-deformation approaches. Our results agree with the exact point-mass solution to $10^{-13}$ and show good agreement with other methods. We present several examples for non-trivial lens models, showing potential for parameter estimation of realistic lenses. The method is particularly efficient when the amplification factor is needed for many source positions. In addition to transient broadband compact binary coalescence GW signals, we apply it to long-duration (continuous), quasi-monochromatic GW signals, for which the relative motion of source and lens makes the amplification factor time-dependent over the observation. The implementation is publicly available in the Python package GWLENS.

</details>

Abstract: Transient noise artifacts ("glitches") in the Laser Interferometer Gravitational-Wave Observatory (LIGO), Virgo and KAGRA (LVK) detectors can mimic astrophysical signals and reduce confidence in gravitational-wave detections. The LVK's iDQ pipeline identifies these glitches by analyzing correlations between auxiliary sensors and the strain channel using the Ordered Veto List (OVL) algorithm. We revisit neural-network approaches to this problem using modern training infrastructure and introduce two architectures: GIANTS, a single network trained on all auxiliary-channel features, and TITAN, a mixture-of-experts model composed of subsystem-specific networks and a learned combiner. Evaluated on ten independent test periods spanning LIGO's third observing run, both models outperform OVL on most test sets, with TITAN providing the most consistent performance over time. We further show that nearly all predictive information contained in Omicron trigger features is captured by a simple binary indicator of auxiliary-channel activity. Networks trained on these binary inputs achieve essentially the same performance as models using the full 8,440-dimensional feature set while reducing the input dimensionality by an order of magnitude. These results demonstrate that modern neural networks provide a robust alternative to OVL and suggest that substantially simpler data representations are sufficient for accurate glitch identification in future iDQ deployments.

Abstract: We study the relaxation of a Robin scalar field near a stable exceptional point of a planar Schwarzschild AdS black hole, where finite boundary driving controls the recovery time without changing the leading decay rate at linear order. At the exceptional point, two weighted moments of the source determine the time dependence of the leading response. When the zeroth moment vanishes while the first remains nonzero, the linear time factor is eliminated, leaving an ordinary exponential decay. With suitable boundary preparation, a perturbation that retains more energy at release can nevertheless reach a common recovery threshold sooner. At the exceptional point, its recovery time advantage over a fixed reference that retains the linear factor grows as $γ_*^{-1}\log\log(1/θ)$ as the common amplitude threshold $θ$ approaches zero, where $γ_*$ is the common decay rate. The source moments also determine the constant term in this asymptotic time difference. Full scalar field evolution on a fixed background near the exceptional point illustrates the reversal for pulses matched in duration, boundary task, source cost, and injected work, within the time window where the small spectral splitting remains unresolved. The injected work also modifies the final spectrum through gravitational heating. When weak driving begins with a black hole at the exceptional point, the temperature rise is quadratic in the source amplitude, whereas the final pole splitting is linear in its magnitude. This splitting limits the time window over which the exceptional point recovery law can be observed.

Abstract: For over two decades, the orbit-averaged precession equations of spinning binary black holes at second post-Newtonian order have been a standard model for their spin dynamics. One of their constants of motion, the projected effective spin, underlies the stability analysis of aligned-spin configurations and the construction of analytical solutions. The system is known to be Hamiltonian and integrable, its Hamiltonian being the orbit average of the full post-Newtonian Hamiltonian. Independently, we derive this Hamiltonian from the equations of motion alone, show why the orbit average gives the correct equations, and relate its constants of motion to those of the full system. We then construct the action-angle variables and frequencies with the recently introduced method of fictitious variables, and propose a criterion for the breakdown of the integrability conditions. This criterion recovers the separatrices of simple classical systems and the up-down instability, whose thresholds we give explicitly for eccentric orbits. We also show that, when all binaries of an ensemble share the same parameters and radiation reaction does not depend on the spins, the Hamiltonian structure preserves an isotropic distribution of spin directions. We confirm numerically that isotropy can be lost when the parameters are correlated with the spin directions. Finally, we use the orbit-averaged Hamiltonian to generalize the projected effective spin to binary neutron stars, for which it is not conserved, and obtain a closed form valid for eccentric orbits.

Abstract: We re-examine the methodology for constraining cosmological first-order phase transitions from gravitational-wave searches using Bayesian statistical analysis. Specifically, we use results from LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA searches for a stochastic gravitational wave background during observing runs O1--O4a. We explore multiple prior choices across three categories of fit parameters: geometrical parameters, thermodynamical parameters of phase transitions, and the parameters of an Abelian Higgs particle physics model. Our key finding is that the constraints on most parameters describing gravitational waves from a phase transition depend strongly on the prior. Consequently, the data do not yet allow to derive robust constraints on these parameters. Besides, even if a parameter is meaningfully constrained, the upper limit can vary significantly with the prior range. *Ye Zhou*

TL;DR: 本文研究了当麦克斯韦不变量乘以任意标量曲率响应函数h(R)时，球对称收缩对称球中电磁通量导致的中心奇异性如何变化；结果表明磁荷与电位移荷不能共存，且在h有界且远离零时两者均不可能存在；磁荷要求h=o(r^4)，电位移荷要求r^4|h|→∞，这些阈值是精确的，并实现光滑几何中心的r^6（磁）和r^{-6}（电）速率；同时分类了零响应情形下电场可能存活但其位移电荷消失的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统球对称情况下电磁通量会导致r^{-4}麦克斯韦应力和中心奇异性，本文旨在探究引入标量曲率响应h(R)后该奇异性障碍的变化机制。

Method: 针对实、静态、球对称解，利用径向与时间向引力方程之差导出复合响应变量的线性方程，并结合迹方程，在Kretschmann标量有界条件下约束响应加权的麦克斯韦源；未预设Ricci标量的极限或导数界。

Result: 磁荷与电位移荷不可共存；若h有界且远离零，则二者均不可能；在各自允许分支上，Ricci标量收敛、加权源趋于零；磁荷需h=o(r^4)，电位移荷需r^4|h|→∞；这些阈值被精确解证实为尖锐，并给出r^6（磁）和r^{-6}（电）的中心行为；零响应情形下可存在无位移电荷的电场。

Conclusion: 标量曲率响应h(R)显著改变球对称电磁系统中心奇异性结构；其渐近行为严格决定是否允许磁/电荷及其几何正则性；零响应区域揭示了电场与位移电荷解耦的可能性。

Abstract: An electric or magnetic flux through shrinking symmetry spheres ordinarily produces an $r^{-4}$ Maxwell stress and a singular center. We examine how this obstruction changes when the Maxwell invariant is multiplied by an arbitrary scalar-curvature response $h(R)$. For real, static, spherically symmetric solutions, the difference between the radial and temporal gravitational equations gives a linear equation for a composite response variable. Combined with the trace equation, it bounds the response-weighted Maxwell source whenever the Kretschmann scalar is bounded, without assuming a limit or derivative bounds for the Ricci scalar. Magnetic and electric displacement charges cannot coexist, and neither charge is possible if $h$ remains bounded and bounded away from zero. On either surviving charged branch, the Ricci scalar converges and the weighted source tends to zero. Magnetic charge requires $h=o(r^4)$, whereas electric displacement requires $r^4|h|\to\infty$. Exact solutions show that these thresholds are sharp and realize the $r^6$ magnetic and $r^{-6}$ electric rates for a smooth geometric center. We also classify the zero-response sector, where an electric field may survive even though its displacement charge vanishes.

</details>


### [99] [Complete quasinormal modes of Type-D black holes. III. Reissner-Nordstrom spectra from zero to extremal charge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24647)
*Changkai Chen,Xiaohua Zhang,Zhoujian Cao,Jiliang Jing*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过解析延拓和零点定位方法，系统研究了Reissner-Nordström黑洞的引力准正规模（QNMs）随电荷变化的行为，揭示了其从Schwarzschild极限到极值极限的演化路径、代数特殊频率附近的物理关联，以及新发现的非常规黎曼面上的模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期计算未能阐明带电黑洞的引力准正规模如何恢复Schwarzschild谱，以及其靠近代数特殊频率的模式与精确特殊解之间的关系。

Method: 将Kerr黑洞分析方法推广至Reissner-Nordström黑洞，采用解析延拓入射 horizon 解并在无穷远定位入射振幅零点；对ℓ=2和3，追踪前42个泛音族从Schwarzschild极限至极值极限的演化；首次在精确极值情形下计算非常规黎曼片上的引力准正规模。

Result: 发现84个泛音族中75个趋于有限阻尼的极值频率，9个趋于零频且激发消失；零电荷时极轴代数特殊模是准正规模，极值电荷时精确轴向特殊模为全透射模；中间电荷下ℓ=3的(22,23)和(23,24)泛音对激发因子共振增强最显著。

Conclusion: 电荷演化轨迹清晰区分了Schwarzschild的非常规模式与邻近分支，并建立了代数特殊频率处QNM与特殊解的统一图像；极值极限下存在新的非常规黎曼面上的准正规模，拓展了黑洞微扰理论的适用范围。

Abstract: Earlier calculations left open how gravitational quasinormal modes (QNMs) of charged black holes recover the Schwarzschild spectrum and how the modes near algebraically special frequencies relate to the exact special solutions. We extend our Kerr analysis to Reissner-Nordström black holes by analytically continuing the ingoing horizon solution and locating the zeros of the incoming amplitude at infinity. For each of $\ell=2$ and $3$, we trace the first 42 overtone families from the Schwarzschild limit to extremality. The charge trajectories distinguish crossings of the negative imaginary axis from turns near it and separate the unconventional Schwarzschild modes from neighboring branches. At zero charge the polar algebraically special mode is a QNM. At extremal charge the exact axial special modes are total-transmission modes. Of the 84 families, 75 approach distinct extremal frequencies with finite damping, and nine approach zero frequency with vanishing excitation. At exact extremality we also compute, for the first time, gravitational QNMs on the unconventional Riemann sheet reached across the negative imaginary axis. At intermediate charge, the clearest resonant enhancement of both excitation factors occurs for the $\ell=3$ overtone pairs $(22,23)$ and $(23,24)$.

</details>


### [100] [Surrogate Modeling and Reduced-Order Methods in Gravitational-Wave Physics and Numerical Relativity: From Spectral Representations to Adaptive Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24727)
*Manuel Tiglio*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文综述了投影式降阶建模（ROM）在引力波天文学中的应用，重点介绍了其数学基础、实际影响、克服近似瓶颈的方法，并展望了与科学机器学习结合的下一代探测器应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波天文学和高精度数值相对论的快速发展导致了计算瓶颈：爱因斯坦场方程的直接数值积分和高维贝叶斯参数估计需要数百万次波形计算，传统求解器难以支撑。

Method: 回顾了基于投影的降阶建模方法（如本征正交分解、贪心基选择、经验插值法），分析其在构建代理波形族和降阶积分中的应用；提出自适应hp-贪心区域划分与多尺度谱压缩以应对Kolmogorov n-宽度衰减缓慢的问题；并融合经认证的降阶结构、科学机器学习与敏感性感知量化。

Result: 建立了可兼顾物理精度与计算效率的高效波形建模框架，为LISA、爱因斯坦望远镜和宇宙探索者等下一代探测器提供了严谨可行的计算蓝图。

Conclusion: 降阶建模不仅是缓解当前计算压力的关键工具，更通过与现代AI和误差认证技术融合，成为支撑未来高精度引力波数据分析的核心范式。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of gravitational-wave astronomy and high-precision numerical relativity has created an acute computational bottleneck: direct numerical integration of the Einstein field equations and high-dimensional Bayesian parameter estimation require millions of waveform evaluations that cannot be sustained by traditional solvers alone. Reduced-order modeling (ROM) and surrogate techniques have emerged as foundational tools to bridge this gap, compressing high-fidelity solution manifolds into compact, rapidly evaluable representations without sacrificing physical accuracy. This perspective reviews the mathematical pillars of projection-based reduced-order modeling---including Proper Orthogonal Decomposition, greedy basis selection, and the Empirical Interpolation Method---and traces their operational impact in gravitational-wave data analysis through surrogate waveform families and Reduced-Order Quadratures. We then examine the fundamental approximation barriers posed by slow Kolmogorov n-width decay in non-stationary, transient, and strongly nonlinear regimes, showing how adaptive hp-greedy domain partitioning and multi-scale spectral compression overcome these limits. Finally, we discuss how combining certified reduced-order structures with modern scientific machine learning and sensitivity-aware quantization provides a rigorous blueprint for next-generation detectors such as LISA, Einstein Telescope, and Cosmic Explorer.

</details>


### [101] [Efficient search for microlensed gravitational-wave signals with matched filtering and a two-image statistic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24895)
*Soumen Roy,Justin Janquart*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的引力波搜索方法，用于有效探测和识别由微透镜效应导致波形畸变的致密双星并合信号，显著降低了信噪比损失，并提升了探测体积和透镜性质识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准匹配滤波搜索使用未透镜化的模板，无法应对微透镜引起的频率相关波形畸变，导致信噪比下降和一致性检验惩罚，从而漏检微透镜信号。

Method: 在PyCBC搜索框架下，通过在模拟数据中注入微透镜信号进行量化分析；利用微透镜信号位于模板及其时移副本张成的二维子空间这一特性，解析最大化相对放大率与Morse相位，仅对时间延迟做搜索，构造新检测统计量。

Result: 改进方法将重加权信噪比的中位损失从10%降至1.6%，90%分位损失从41%降至9%；日虚警率下整体相对探测体积提升约6%，对受透镜影响最严重的10%信号提升达1.9倍；同时该统计量可用于判定触发事件是否为透镜事件。

Conclusion: 所提方法无需扩展模板库，即可显著提升微透镜引力波信号的探测效率与识别能力，为实际数据分析中开展微透镜信号搜寻奠定了基础。

Abstract: Gravitational waves from compact binary coalescences can be microlensed by compact objects along the line of sight, producing frequency-dependent waveform distortions. As standard matched-filter searches rely on unlensed templates without accounting for lensing effects, microlensed signals can lose signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) through template mismatch and suffer further losses from signal-consistency tests. We quantify both effects for the $\texttt{PyCBC}$ search using microlensed injections in simulated data. The two losses occupy different regions of lens parameter space in the geometric optics representation: template mismatch is governed by the relative magnification of the two images, while the consistency-test penalty is governed by their time delay. We show that a microlensed signal lies in the two-dimensional subspace spanned by a template and its time-shifted copy, allowing the relative magnification and Morse phase to be maximized over analytically, leaving only the time delay to search. The resulting statistic requires no extension of the template bank and reduces the median loss in re-weighted SNR from $10\%$ to $1.6\%$, and the $90^\mathrm{th}$ percentile from $41\%$ to $9\%$. At a false-alarm rate of one per day, the relative detection volume improves by about $6\%$ across all injections, and by a factor of $1.9$ for the $10\%$ of injections most affected by lensing. In addition to increased detection capacities, we also show how the detection statistic can be used to establish the lensed nature of the trigger. Therefore, the work proposed here lays the foundation for an effective search and identification of microlensed signals in gravitational-wave data.

</details>

### [102] [Commuting integrals of motion for free bosonic CFTs revisited: certified densities, spectral rigidity, and an exact identification at the c=1 branch point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23237)
