TL;DR: 本文重新研究了自由玻色CFT中由hep-th/0404195构造的对易局域守恒量，利用精确算符积引擎，通过引导引理将过定双线性对易系统简化为一个特殊奇数荷D加线性核问题；对Z₂×Z₂“回形针”族和A₂⁽²⁾族分别验证了低自旋对易性、给出了精确库仑气体字典，并确认其与量子KdV层级的关系；在c=1时确认所有奇自旋荷即量子KdV荷，且排除了非KdV候选者；最后提出一个关于双线性系统秩亏与筛选核特征标关联的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视自由玻色CFT中已知的对易局域守恒量（如hep-th/0404195所构建），旨在以高精度、可验证方式厘清其结构、对易性、谱归属及与已知可积模型（如量子KdV、affine-Toda）的严格对应关系，并澄清此前未严格证明或存在歧义的陈述。

Method: 采用基于精确算术的算符积引擎进行符号计算；引入‘bootstrap引理’将过定双线性对易条件约化为含奇数荷D的线性核问题；对不同对称性族（Z₂×Z₂、A₂⁽²⁾、Z₂×O(N)）分别开展机器验证（有限范围）、解析证明（如对易恒等式）、字典构造（Coulomb-gas映射）及退化分析；严格区分‘全自旋已证’与‘有限范围验证’结论。

Result: 1）对Z₂×Z₂族：给出自旋-7密度P₈，严格证明[I₃,I₅]=[I₃,I₇]=[I₅,I₇]=0对任意n成立，并验证LVZ四筛选刻画至自旋9；2）对A₂⁽²⁾族：建立精确Coulomb-gas字典连接Φ₁,₂/Φ₂,₁扰动，从而导出全部6n±1自旋守恒量；3）在c=1时确认所有奇自旋荷即量子KdV荷（验证至自旋15），且排除非KdV候选者，识别自对偶退化为L(1,0)；4）对Z₂×O(N)三族，确认I₃中心化子在自旋≤11时奇自旋一维、偶自旋零维，并发现N=-2处纤维跃变；5）校验并修正2004年低自旋公式（5处修正，1处待定）；6）提出双线性系统秩亏与筛选核特征标关联的corank-character猜想。

Conclusion: 自由玻色CFT中的对易局域守恒量结构比以往更清晰：其核心由一个基本奇数荷D与线性核决定；多个著名族（Z₂×Z₂、A₂⁽²⁾、Z₂×O(N)）在不同参数下均嵌入量子KdV或affine-Toda层级，但需依赖精确字典与机器辅助验证；‘全自旋’结论必须有严格证明支撑，而有限验证结果亦具高度可靠性；最终提出的corank-character猜想为理解守恒量生成机制提供了新代数视角。

Abstract: We revisit the commuting local integrals of motion for free bosonic CFTs constructed in hep-th/0404195 as input data for integrable boundary states, using exact-arithmetic operator-product engines. A bootstrap lemma reduces its overdetermined bilinear commutativity systems to a distinguished odd charge D plus linear kernel problems -- conditionally on even-spin vacancy, a machine-verified but finite-range input. For the Z_2 x Z_2 "paperclip" family we publish the spin-7 density P_8, prove [I_3,I_5]=[I_3,I_7]=[I_5,I_7]=0 identically in n, and verify the Lukyanov-Vitchev-Zamolodchikov four-screening characterization at spins 7 and 9 at sampled n. For the A_2^(2) family we give an exact Coulomb-gas dictionary to the dual Phi_{1,2}/Phi_{2,1} perturbations (that identification itself being classical, Zamolodchikov; Smirnov), whence the infinite hierarchy at all spins 6n+-1 follows by the Feigin-Frenkel affine-Toda theorem; our contribution is the certified dictionary, not a new all-spin construction. At c=1 the tower's defining charges are exactly quantum KdV at c_{1,4}=-25/2 -- the all-odd sector of the Freeman-Hornfeck-West family: the hierarchy embeds at every odd spin, equality verified through spin 15, all-spin multiplicity one open. We exclude or disfavor the named non-KdV candidates at the tested loci and identify the self-dual degeneracy as L(1,0). Through spin 11, the generic centralizer of I_3 is, for all three Z_2 x O(N) "cylindrical" families, one-dimensional at odd and zero at even spins, with Q(t)-symbolic charges at spins 7, 9, 11 and an explained fiber jump at N=-2. We certify the published 2004 low-spin formulas and record five corrections (one presumed). All-spin statements are made only where proved, finite-range otherwise. We close with a corank-character conjecture linking the rank deficiencies of the bilinear systems to characters of screening kernels.

</details>


### [103] [Exact Solutions to Matrix Models and String Theories: The Global Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23366)
*Jasper Kager,João Rodrigues,Ricardo Schiappa,Maximilian Schwick,Noam Tamarin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过离散傅里叶/Zak变换表示，得到了矩阵模型与弦理论的精确非微扰解，并系统构建了完整的非线性Stokes数据与连接公式，揭示了本征值与反本征值（负张力D膜）在谱曲线多叶结构中的隧穿行为及其在相图全局分析中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为获得矩阵模型与弦理论的全局、精确非微扰解，需克服传统微扰方法在Stokes线穿越、相变区域及强耦合/小N极限下的失效问题，并统一描述D膜与负张力D膜的贡献。

Method: 采用离散傅里叶/Zak变换表征配分函数的 resurgence transseries；系统研究（反）本征值在多叶谱曲线上的隧穿；推导完整非线性Stokes数据与连接公式；结合大阶分析与矩阵积分进行验证。

Result: 获得了所有厄米单矩阵模型及其拓扑弦对偶、多临界与极小弦双标度极限的闭式非微扰解；构建了全参数（复参数、任意N、任意't Hooft耦合）下有效的Stokes自动同构；数值验证显示本征值与反本征值共存对精度至关重要。

Conclusion: 反本征值（负张力D膜）不是可选修正项，而是实现全局精确非微扰解的必要组成部分；本文建立的Stokes框架为理解量子引力、规范/弦对偶及非微扰相变提供了普适工具。

Abstract: Exact nonperturbative solutions to matrix models and string theories are obtained in closed-form via a discrete-Fourier/Zak-transform representation of their partition-function resurgent-transseries. Examples include all hermitian one-matrix models (paired with their topological string duals) alongside all their multicritical and minimal-string double-scaling limits. The resulting structure is resonant, necessarily including both eigenvalues (D-branes) and anti-eigenvalues (negative-tension-branes). In a complementary preceding paper these solutions were extensively verified at the local level. In order to study them globally across their phase diagrams, complete non-linear Stokes data (and corresponding connection formulae) are still needed -- all of which are obtained herein. This involves a careful study of (anti-) eigenvalue tunneling across the complete multi-sheeted structure of their associated spectral-curves, and how the result descends from multi-cut smooth-curves to single-cut singular-curves -- all of which are extensively checked via large-order analysis and matrix-integral computations. Making use of the resulting exact Stokes automorphisms, nonperturbative solutions can be fully tested, from weak-to-strong 't Hooft coupling, as well as from large-to-small N, and for any complex-values of all parameters, by correctly implementing Stokes-transitions at the crossing of all possible Stokes-lines. Several analytical and numerical tests are carried through in diverse examples covering from matrix models through non-critical strings, which produce very sharp numerical matches fully validating our exact analytical expressions. Anti-eigenvalues or negative-tension D-branes are an absolutely essential part of the story in order to find such precise agreements.

</details>


### [104] [Localizing the M-Theory Path Integral: Membrane Instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23369)
*Pietro Benetti Genolini,Jerome P. Gauntlett,Jaeha Park,James Sparks*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算M理论路径积分中M2-膜瞬子贡献的公式，包含了对普朗克长度所有阶的量子修正，并在ABJM和V^{5,2}理论背景下验证了该公式的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为精确计算M理论中M2-膜瞬子对路径积分的贡献，尤其是包含全阶量子修正，并与对偶场论（如ABJM）的瞬子结果一致。

Method: 利用相对等变局域化确定M2-膜作用量，用等变Dolbeault特征计算单圈行列式，并对连续M2-膜族在其模空间上应用等变局域化。

Result: 在ABJM对偶背景中成功复现已知场论瞬子系数，并给出质量与压扁形变在任意陈-西蒙斯水平下的新解析预言；对V^{5,2}理论首次计算了瞬子贡献，并建立了其在特殊压扁参数下与ABJM球面配分函数的精确关系。

Conclusion: 所提公式统一处理M2-瞬子及其量子修正，不仅验证了AdS/CFT对应中的瞬子匹配，还为更广义超对称背景提供了可计算框架。

Abstract: We propose a formula for M2-instanton contributions to the M-theory path integral, incorporating quantum corrections to all orders in the Planck length. The M2-brane action is determined by relative equivariant localization, while an equivariant Dolbeault character computes the one-loop determinant. Continuous families of M2-branes are treated by equivariant localization on their moduli spaces. For M-theory backgrounds dual to ABJM theory, we compute single instanton contributions from linear holomorphic fillings to the sphere partition function and the topologically twisted index associated with supersymmetric $AdS_4$ black holes. Our results reproduce known field theory instanton coefficients and give new analytic predictions for mass and squashing deformations at all Chern-Simons levels. For the $V^{5,2}$ theory, we compute new instanton contributions and establish an exact relation between its partition function at special squashing and that of ABJM theory on the round three-sphere.

</details>


### [105] [A Positive Metric on Four-Dimensional QFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23370)
*Thomas Hartman,Grégoire Mathys,Andreas Stergiou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了Jack-Osborn度量的非微扰推广，并证明其非负性由平均零能量条件（ANEC）保证，从而导出强a定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立四维量子场论空间上的自然度量，并理解a-反常流与a定理的非微扰基础。

Method: 基于对三点半函数⟨OI E OJ⟩的谱分析，其中OI为相关算符、E为平均零能量；在固定光射线的零旋转谱下分解该三点半函数，得到各冲击参数下的正定性条件。

Result: 得到了Jack-Osborn度量的非微扰推广；证明该度量非负依赖于ANEC；ANEC由此蕴含强a定理，并提供流上的单调函数。

Conclusion: ANEC是强a定理的物理基础，且该工作将微扰结果推广至非微扰框架，强化了量子场论中RG流的几何与因果结构理解。

Abstract: A natural metric on the space of four-dimensional quantum field theories was introduced by Jack and Osborn in classic work on the local renormalization group. In perturbation theory, the Jack-Osborn metric determines the flow of the $a$-anomaly, and positivity implies the $a$-theorem. We derive a non-perturbative generalization of the Jack-Osborn metric and show that it is non-negative by the averaged null energy condition (ANEC). Consequently, the ANEC implies the strong $a$-theorem, and provides the monotonic function along the flow. The method is based on spectral analysis of the three-point function $\langle \mathcal{O}_I \mathcal{E} \mathcal{O}_J\rangle$, where the $\mathcal{O}_I$ are relevant operators and $\mathcal{E}$ is the averaged null energy. This three-point function is decomposed under the spectrum of null rotations that fix the light ray, leading to a positivity condition at each impact parameter.

</details>


### [106] [Horizon kinematics and the fine structure induced by finite-energy Hawking radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23376)
*M. A. Aguirre Cortés,Moisés Bravo-Gaete,Julio A. Méndez-Zavaleta,Efraín Rojas Marcial*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过量子隧穿方法研究霍金辐射对黑洞视界结构的影响，提出了一种在有限能量辐射下重构视界位移的系统性策略，并以非线性带电BTZ黑洞为例，揭示了内视界分裂、退化保持/恢复、趋近极值态等新现象及其热力学与几何行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 霍金辐射会通过质量与荷的损失改变黑洞视界结构，但有限能量辐射下的视界演化（尤其当视界半径无显式解时）缺乏系统分析工具；需发展不依赖显式根表达式的几何追踪方法。

Method: 基于视界条件的反演，沿静态几何序列追踪视界根的位移；结合非线性带电BTZ黑洞模型，分析中性与带电辐射下内视界拓扑变化、退化行为及通向极值态的动力学路径；辅以半经典隧穿概率与几何展开分析。

Result: 1）中性辐射必然导致双重内视界分裂为两个单重根，暴露视界精细结构；2）带电辐射可通过电荷-能量关系调控退化是否保持或恢复，并存在多条具有相同主导半经典因子的内部演化路径；3）持续辐射可使较大内视界与事件视界合并，系统趋近零温极值态，但有限边界时间内不可达；4）在特定参数区，几何展开与热近似仍可控；5）量子反作用修正使温度升高、中性探针辐射因子增强。

Conclusion: 视界演化具有丰富的拓扑与动力学结构，不能仅由渐近态刻画；半经典框架下，有限能量辐射过程蕴含非平凡的内部历史多样性与几何-热力学协同行为，为理解黑洞微观演化与信息问题提供新视角。

Abstract: Hawking radiation can modify the horizon structure of a black hole through the loss of mass and global charges. Investigating this process through quantum tunneling, we develop a systematic strategy to reconstruct horizon displacements under finite-energy emission, even when their radii are not explicitly known. Inversion of the horizon condition allows to follow roots's displacements along sequences of static geometries. As a concrete realization, we consider a nonlinearly charged extension of the Bañados--Teitelboim--Zanelli (BTZ) family, whose horizon structure offers an ideal setup to explore this process. Of particular interest is an initial configuration with a double inner horizon coexisting with a simple event horizon. We show that neutral emission necessarily splits the inner root into two simple ones, revealing a horizon fine structure. For charged emission, the dependence of extracted charge on emitted energy determines the separation law and allows that preservation or later restore the degeneracy. We construct such channels connecting the same initial and final states through distinct internal histories sharing the same leading semiclassical factor. As emission continues, the larger of the newly separated inner horizons can coalesce with the event horizon, driving the sequence towards extremality with a vanishing temperature but finite tunneling probability. We find that zero temperature cannot be attained in finite boundary time while uniform adiabaticity bounds hold. We also establish a regime where the geometric expansion and the canonical thermal approximation remain controlled near this endpoint. Finally, applying this analysis to the sector reproducing a quantum-backreacted BTZ geometry reveals how the leading semiclassical corrections raise the temperature and enhance the neutral-probe horizon emission factor relative to classical BTZ.

</details>


### [107] [The Holomorphic-Topological Poisson Sigma Model and Quantization of Affine W-algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23377)
*Niklas Garner,Brian R. Williams,Paul-Hermann Balduf*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用Khan和Zeng提出的全纯-拓扑泊松sigma模型，显式构造了所有单李代数𝔤的经典主仿射W-代数的形变量子化；通过线性微分分级表示将体理论识别为近主幂零背景下的陈-西蒙斯理论，并得到有限、单圈精确的量子化；边界上的量子算子可观测量构成水平为k−h∨ℏ的主仿射W-代数；对𝔰𝔩₂情形，还证明了陈-西蒙斯理论与三维手征引力在量子层面的微扰等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为经典主仿射W-代数提供一种基于物理场论（特别是全纯-拓扑泊松sigma模型）的显式形变量子化构造，并建立其与陈-西蒙斯理论及手征引力的深层量子联系。

Method: 运用Khan-Zeng的全纯-拓扑泊松sigma模型；采用线性微分分级表示将体理论映射到主幂零背景下的Chern-Simons理论；利用全纯规范实现有限且单圈精确的量子化；分析量子边界可观测量并导出W-代数及其水平偏移；对𝔰𝔩₂情形进行微扰量子等价性证明。

Result: 成功构造任意单李代数𝔤的主仿射W-代数的显式形变量子化；确认量子边界可观测量即为水平k−h∨ℏ的主仿射W-代数；直接导出仿射电流代数的水平偏移；对𝔰𝔩₂情形严格证明Chern-Simons理论与三维手征引力的量子等价性及其参数对应关系。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了经典W-代数量子化与拓扑/全纯场论之间的系统性桥梁，不仅给出了统一的构造框架，还揭示了W-代数、Chern-Simons理论与量子引力之间深刻的统一结构。

Abstract: We construct explicit deformation quantizations of classical principal affine $W$-algebras for every simple Lie algebra $\mathfrak g$ using the holomorphic-topological Poisson sigma model of A. Khan and K. Zeng. A linear differential-graded presentation identifies the universal bulk with Chern--Simons theory near a principal nilpotent background, where holomorphic gauge gives a finite, 1-loop exact quantization. The quantum oper boundary observables form the principal affine $W$-algebra at level $k-h^\vee\hbar$; we also obtain this level shift directly for affine current algebras. For $\mathfrak{sl}_2$, we prove a perturbative quantum equivalence between Chern--Simons theory and three-dimensional chiral gravity, including their oper and Dirichlet boundary conditions, and determine the quantum relation between their parameters.

</details>


### [108] [Melvin-type Electromagnetized Kerr Black Hole in Kaluza-Klein and Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton-axion Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23379)
*Liang Ma,Zhi-Chao Li,H. Lu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文改进了求解生成技术，构造了无Taubs-旋涡项的Melvin型电磁化Kerr黑洞精确解，并推广至KK理论和EMDA理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 避免在构造Melvin型旋转黑洞时引入不期望的Taub-旋涡项。

Method: 修改电磁化过程，消除Taub-旋涡项，独立引入对偶电荷与外电磁场。

Result: 得到了KK理论和EMDA理论中无旋涡项的Melvin型电磁化Kerr黑洞精确解。

Conclusion: 改进后的解生成技术可避免Taubs-NUT或Taubs-旋涡扭曲，适用于Ricci平坦种子度规中独立引入对偶电荷和外电磁场。

Abstract: A naive application of solution-generating techniques to construct Melvin-type rotating black holes in the Kaluza-Klein (KK) theory in the presence of both electric and magnetic external fields generically introduces an undesirable Taub-swirling twist term. We investigate its origin and modify the electromagnetization procedure, thereby constructing an exact solution of the Melvin-type electromagnetized Kerr black hole in the KK theory without the swirling term. We also obtain the analogous solution in the Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton-axion (EMDA) theory. The modified solution-generating techniques allow one to introduce both the dyonic charges and external electromagnetic fields independently into Ricci-flat seed metrics in both theories, without generating either Taub-NUT or Taub-swirling twists.

</details>


### [109] [Non-Perturbative Moduli Stabilization with Sp$(4,\mathbb Z)$ Modular Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23381)
*Miguel Hernandez-Segura,Xiang-Gan Liu,Hans Peter Nilles,Saul Ramos-Sanchez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在杂化弦理论的N=1超引力模型中，利用胶子凝聚稳定与Sp(4,Z)模对称性相关的三个复模量的问题，通过一阶阈值修正固定了超势的模权重和奇点结构，并基于Sp(4,Z)基本模不变比及其相容关系，给出了各类阈值下一致超势的完整参数化，从而支持丰富的超对称闵可夫斯基真空景观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弦理论中，模量稳定是构建现实物理模型的关键问题；而以往基于SL(2,Z)对称性的方法不足以处理更复杂的Sp(4,Z)情形，因此需要发展更普适的模不变构造技术。

Method: 利用Sp(4,Z)的基本模不变比及其syzygies（相容关系）系统构造超势，结合一阶阈值修正确定其模权重与奇点结构，并分析不同真空（包括固定点与非固定点）下的模量稳定情况。

Result: 发现高阶零点可导致连续的超对称闵可夫斯基真空（曲面或曲线）；六个孤立不动点中仅具S4和D12剩余对称性的点能完全稳定三个模量；还构造出若干远离不动点的孤立真空，部分与属二模味拟合所偏好的模量值一致，并观察到增强对称点附近几何模量质量被压低的现象。

Conclusion: Sp(4,Z)模对称性为模量稳定提供了比SL(2,Z)更丰富、更灵活的结构，支持多样化的真空解，尤其在连接模味物理与弦紧化方面具有重要潜力。

Abstract: We study the stabilization of the three complex moduli associated with Sp$(4,\mathbb Z)$ modular symmetry by gaugino condensation in $\mathcal N=1$ supergravity models from heterotic string theory, where one-loop threshold corrections fix both the modular weight and the singularity structure of the superpotential. We determine, for each threshold class, the complete parametrization of the consistent superpotential relying on Sp$(4,\mathbb Z)$ basic modular-invariant ratios and their syzygies, generalizing the insufficient SL$(2,\mathbb Z)$-inspired techniques based solely on polynomials of its absolute invariants. This structure supports a rich landscape of supersymmetric Minkowski vacua. Unlike in the SL$(2,\mathbb Z)$ case, higher-order zeros produce continuous surfaces and curves of supersymmetric Minkowski vacua, while, among the six isolated fixed points, only those with $S_4$ and $D_{12}$ residual symmetries can stabilize all three moduli. We also construct isolated vacua away from fixed points, some of which lie at the moduli values preferred by genus-two modular flavor fits, and examine the suppression of the geometric moduli masses near an enhanced-symmetry point.

</details>


### [110] [Scalar/axion Wormholes and Boundary Conditions in Gravitational Path Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23392)
*Jason Hallsten,Jesse Held,Zhenyu Heng,Donald Marolf,Zhencheng Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了不同边界条件（狄利克雷和诺伊曼）下，各类时空虫洞对引力路径积分贡献的差异，并在多种简化模型中验证了这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明，特定类别的时空虫洞是否对引力路径积分有贡献，强烈依赖于所选边界条件；本文旨在系统研究这种依赖性。

Method: 通过构建并分析三种简化模型——JT引力耦合标量场、渐近AdS引力耦合轴子/标量场（环面边界）、渐近平直引力耦合轴子/标量场——在低能有效理论框架下，分别计算狄利克雷（Z_D）与诺伊曼（Z_N）边界条件下的配分函数，并比较其鞍点贡献。

Result: 在所有模型中均发现，尽管Z_D与Z_N由积分变换关联，但二者通常由不同的鞍点（即不同虫洞构型）主导；同时揭示了半经典计算中若干新奇细微问题。

Conclusion: 边界条件的选择实质性地改变了虫洞对路径积分的贡献结构，表明在量子引力中边界条件不仅是技术细节，更可能具有物理本质意义。

Abstract: Recent work noted that whether or not a given class of spacetime wormholes contributes to the gravitational path integral can depend strongly on the choice of boundary conditions. In particular, for asymptotically-AdS scalar/axion wormholes with spherical boundaries it was suggested that there are regimes where the same wormhole contributes to a Euclidean low-energy effective field theory computation of the partition function $Z_N$ defined by imposing Neumann boundary conditions on the scalar/axion field, while it fails to contribute to a corresponding partition function $Z_D$ defined by imposing Dirichlet boundary conditions on this field. Here we investigate toy models of related wormholes associated with JT gravity coupled to a scalar field, asymptotically-AdS gravity coupled to an axion/scalar with toroidal boundary conditions, and asymptotically flat gravity coupled to an axion/scalar. In all cases we compare $Z_D$ and $Z_N$ as computed in a truncated low-energy effective theory by an appropriate integral over constrained wormholes. In each case we find that, even though $Z_D$ and $Z_N$ are related by an integral transform, the two generally receive contributions from differing sets of saddles. Along the way, we uncover a variety of additional interesting subtleties associated with semiclassical contributions to our $Z_D$ and $Z_N$.

</details>


### [111] [ER=EPR and IMP wormholes in quantum gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23396)
*Eyoab Bahiru*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过研究IMP虫洞在QFT和量子引力中的表现，特别是在AdS/CFT框架下，对ER=EPR猜想进行了分析与改进，并构建了相关背景下的微扰理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在深化对ER=EPR猜想的理解，利用IMP虫洞这一具体模型，在AdS/CFT背景下探索量子纠缠与时空几何之间的联系。

Method: 采用共形方法构造IMP虫洞解，并在此基础上发展适用于渐近AdS时空的量子引力微扰理论。

Result: 成功构建了IMP虫洞背景下的微扰理论，并在GN=0极限、微扰小量及非微扰情形下探讨了ER=EPR关系。

Conclusion: IMP虫洞为理解ER=EPR提供了有力工具，共形方法不仅可用于构造经典解，还能支撑起一致的量子引力微扰框架。

Abstract: We analyze and refine the ER=EPR conjecture by studying IMP (Isenberg, Mazzeo and Pollack \cite{Isenberg:2001qe}) wormholes in QFT and quantum gravity, in the AdS/CFT context. IMP wormholes are solutions to the Einstein equations constructed from another general solution of Einstein's equations by gluing a handle on a Cauchy slice with a constant mean curvature, so that the initial data on the slice does not change in the regions away from the handle. This is made possible by using what is called the conformal method. This same method also enables one to recover a well defined perturbation theory in quantum gravity, and particularly for asymptotically AdS spacetimes \cite{Witten:2022xxp}. Thus we construct the perturbation theory on these backgrounds in addition to exploring ER=EPR when $G_{N}=0$, in the limit it is perturbatively small and beyond perturbation theory.

</details>


### [112] [Folded out-of-time-order correlators in double-scaled SYK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23453)
*Joseph D. Lykken,Madeline Mooney,Maria Spiropulu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在DSSYK模型中实现了Harlow-Zhao提出的欧氏时间“折叠”方法，验证了宇宙学视界（如dS空间）中“反混沌”（anti-scrambling）现象的存在：折叠后的OTOC表现出时间提前而非延迟，与AdS中的冲击波行为相反，并与de Sitter eikonal公式一致；同时讨论了其与两壳模型、no-go约束及有限温度下反Stokes相变的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解宇宙学视界（特别是de Sitter空间）的量子信息特征，尤其是区别于黑洞视界的‘反混沌’行为，并探索其在可解量子模型（DSSYK）中的实现机制。

Method: 基于DSSYK模型，采用arXiv:2303.03474定义的无限温OTOC，在小双标度参数λ的半经典极限下复现AdS冲击波行为；再应用Harlow-Zhao的欧氏时间‘折叠’操作（以假逆温β_f代替β），结合数值与解析分析研究折叠后OTOC的动力学。

Result: 折叠OTOC明确呈现反混沌特征（时间提前）；精确DSSYK结果在混沌区匹配de Sitter eikonal公式；虚部再现两壳模型中膨胀项的指数，实部仅O(λ)偏移；有限温下低温时出现反Stokes交叉，抑制eikonal行为提取。

Conclusion: DSSYK模型成功实现了de Sitter宇宙学视界的反混沌信号，支持Harlow-Zhao的折叠方案；结果既符合现有理论约束（如no-go定理），也揭示了有限温度下的新动力学机制（如复共轭鞍点主导与反Stokes现象）。

Abstract: Harlow and Zhao arXiv:2607.14215 have identified ``anti-scrambling'' as a characteristic signature of cosmological horizons in de Sitter space, and proposed that it can be realized in a quantum system with bounded energy by Euclidean time ``folding" of out-of-time-order correlators (OTOCs). We implement this prescription in the DSSYK model. We use arXiv:2303.03474 to define an infinite-temperature ($β=0$) DSSYK OTOC that in the semiclassical limit of small values of the double-scaling parameter $λ$ reproduces the usual AdS eikonal shockwave behavior with its characteristic time delay, and then apply the Harlow-Zhao fold with the ``fake'' DSSYK inverse temperature $β_f$ playing the role of $β$. We demonstrate both numerically and analytically that the resulting folded OTOC displays anti-scrambling. The exact DSSYK evaluation tracks the semiclassical de Sitter eikonal formula in the scrambling regime, including a time advance instead of a time delay.The direction of the fold selects the side from which the continued eikonal amplitude approaches its branch cut. We show how our results are consistent with recent ``no-go" constraints. We compare the folded correlator with the two-shell analysis of arXiv:2607.14215: the growing term of the two-shell dilaton invariant appears in DSSYK with the same exponent, but as an imaginary part, while its real part, which would signal the backreaction that ends in a crunch, only moves by $O(λ)$ for thermal-scale probes. We also generalize to finite $β$; we find reasonable agreement with the de Sitter eikonal form for sufficiently high temperatures, but at lower temperatures the folded correlator is dominated by a pair of complex conjugate saddles, an anti-Stokes crossover that prevents retrieval of the eikonal behavior from the folded OTOC.

</details>


### [113] [Holomorphic Feynman diagrams in four dimensions and the quantum BPS ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24160)
*Botao Li,Kangning Liu,Chi-Ming Chang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在四维N=1超对称规范理论的全纯扭结框架下，系统计算了所有圈阶的全纯费曼图核，揭示其电流方程等效于电阻网络；通过施温格参数与全纯动量的类比，利用串并联及星-三角变换导出各类核，并将扭结微分等同于量子修正的超对称变换；进一步完整给出N=4杨-米尔斯理论中所有算符（含任意全纯导数）的超对称变换修正，构造并证明量子算符乘积在Q-上同调下满足结合律、分次交换律和单位性，从而建立量子BPS环；最后通过计算机普查，在多种变形理论中发现不同圈数的提升现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解超对称规范理论中量子化与扭结操作的相容性，以及复合算符在量子水平上的超对称变换与代数结构（如BPS环）的精确实现。

Method: 基于全纯扭结框架，将费曼图积分核建模为电网络，用施温格参数作为电阻、全纯动量作为注入电流；应用系列网络约化（串、并、星-三角）推导各阶核；借助Gaiotto-Kulp-Wu的标记高阶运算构造量子乘积；结合计算机辅助普查分析不同理论中的算符提升现象。

Result: 给出了N=4 Yang-Mills中所有算符的全阶超对称变换修正；严格证明了量子算符乘积在Q-上同调下构成结合、分次交换、含单位元的代数（即量子BPS环）；发现多类理论中存在一至四圈的算符提升（lift），尤其在N=4及其形变中找到新幸运类与一/两圈提升，但在部分扇区未见两圈提升。

Conclusion: 扭结与量子化可交换；全纯扭结理论能系统描述超对称算符的量子修正与代数结构；量子BPS环具有坚实的微扰构造基础；不同理论中算符提升的存在性揭示了隐藏的量子对称性与结构约束。

Abstract: We compute, at all loop orders, the kernels of the holomorphic Feynman graphs that carry the loop corrections to supersymmetry transformations and to regularized products of local operators in the holomorphic twist of four-dimensional $\mathcal N=1$ supersymmetric gauge theories. The current equations of these holomorphic graph integrals are those of an electrical network. Schwinger parameters are resistances and holomorphic momenta are injected currents. Iterating series, parallel and star--triangle reductions on Schwinger space gives the fan, spider and cubic product-forest kernels at all loop orders. Assuming that twisting commutes with quantization for these composite operators, we identify the twisted differential with their loop-corrected supersymmetry transformation. We give every perturbative correction to the supersymmetry transformation in $\mathcal N=4$ Yang--Mills theory, including its action on operators with arbitrary holomorphic derivatives. Using the marked higher operations of Gaiotto, Kulp and Wu, we also construct and compute the quantum product of operators and prove that, on $\mathbf Q$-cohomology, it is associative, graded commutative and unital, supplying the multiplication of the quantum BPS ring. Every multi-graviton operator thus has an explicit finite completion to a cocycle of the full quantum supercharge. Our computer census finds new fortuitous classes and one-loop lifts in $\mathcal N=4$ Yang--Mills theory with several gauge groups. We have found no two-loop lift in the sectors tested. We also find two-loop lifts in the Leigh--Strassler and $β$-deformations of $\mathcal N=4$ Yang--Mills theory. In other theories, we find two-, three- and four-loop lifts in abelian gauge theories, and two- and three-loop lifts in $\mathcal N=2$ $SU(2)$ and $SU(3)$ gauge theories and their singlet deformations.

</details>


### [114] [Grade Restriction Rule For D-Brane Transport In Non-Abelian Gauged Linear Sigma Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24250)
*Richard Eager,Kentaro Hori,Johanna Knapp,Mauricio Romo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了非阿贝尔规范线性σ模型中B型D-膜的低能行为，重点是确定D-膜沿路径从一个相变到另一个相时的等级限制规则；通过分析半球配分函数积分的收敛条件，在具有弱耦合与强耦合两个相（均为Calabi-Yau三维流形）的具体模型中，结合特殊Chan-Paton表示与‘禁止进入区’，唯一确定了等级限制规则，并修正了原有准则以满足Weyl不变性要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定非阿贝尔GLSM中B型D-膜在相变路径上的等级限制规则，以理解其低能行为及相间输运机制。

Method: 分析半球配分函数积分的收敛条件；识别特殊Chan-Paton表示及其在相边界奇点附近的临界行为；定义并利用‘禁止进入区’刻画收敛轮廓的失效区域；结合Weyl不变性要求修正等级限制准则；将结果应用于超对称QCD边界条件分析。

Result: 成功唯一确定了等级限制规则；发现特殊Chan-Paton表示主导奇点附近的单值性；揭示了‘禁止进入区’对轮廓选择的关键约束；修正了等级限制准则以满足Weyl不变性；获得超对称QCD中精确边界条件，并用于理解强耦合相中D-膜的低能行为。

Conclusion: 等级限制规则由特殊Chan-Paton表示和对应‘禁止进入区’共同决定；Weyl不变性要求对收敛轮廓的约束 necessitates修正原有准则；该方法具有一般性，已成功推广至其他模型。

Abstract: We study the low energy behaviour of B-type D-branes in non-Abelian gauged linear sigma models. The main problem is to determine the grade restriction rule for D-brane transport from one phase to another along paths. We analyze the convergence condition of the integral for the hemisphere partition function. We perform a thorough analysis in a particular model with two phases, one weakly coupled and one strongly coupled, both corresponding to Calabi-Yau threefolds. General methods are developed in this study and are applied to other models successfully. The integrals for a set of special Chan-Paton representations exhibit critical behaviour around each singular point on the phase boundary. From each of these points emanate ``no entry zones'' where a convergent contour is lost or is forced to traverse Gamma function poles. Knowledge of the special representations and no entry zones is strong enough to uniquely determine the grade restriction rule. In the course of this study, we encounter a problem in Weyl invariance of convergent contours, which forces us to modify the grade restriction criterion. The special representations play an important rôle in the monodromy around the singular point. We also obtain exact results on boundary conditions in supersymmetric QCDs and apply them to understand the low energy behaviour of D-branes in the strong coupling phase.

</details>


### [115] [Only the disk: topological obstructions for warped AdS$_6\times S^2$ in Type IIB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24320)
*Nakwoo Kim*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了IIB型超引力中具有超对称AdS6×S2结构的解，这些解由黎曼曲面Σ上的两个全纯微分决定，并在边界极点处存在五维膜；通过分析微分的零点、相位旋转及极点分布等约束，证明Σ必须是单位圆盘，且在无七维膜和定向面时该结论严格成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解IIB型超引力中高维AdS背景的超对称解结构，特别是其几何实现与膜源分布的关系，以推进AdS/CFT对应在非标准维度（如六维）中的应用。

Method: 利用全纯微分的解析性质、相位绕行行为、零点计数定理以及黎曼曲面的双曲几何，结合正则性条件与边界极点约束，推导曲面拓扑限制；引入七维膜与定向面作为内点奇点后，附加全局单值性与奇点渐近行为假设进行推广分析。

Result: 证明在无七维膜和定向面时，支撑超对称AdS6×S2解的黎曼曲面Σ必为圆盘；含相互局域七维膜与定向面时，在附加合理假设下仍得相同结论，且圆盘上至多容许一个定向面奇点；进一步，在无奇点圆盘上，极点间相位变化界可推出五维膜电荷沿边界呈循环序排列。

Conclusion: AdS6×S2超对称解的底流形拓扑被严格限制为圆盘，揭示了高维超引力解中几何、全纯结构与源分布之间的深刻统一性；该结果为构造相应六维共形场论提供了关键几何约束。

Abstract: Supersymmetric AdS$_6\times S^2$ solutions of Type IIB supergravity are warped products over a Riemann surface $Σ$. They are determined by two holomorphic differentials on $Σ$, with five-branes at poles on its boundary. We derive restrictions on the genus and the number of boundary components of $Σ$. Without seven-branes or orientifolds, the differentials are taken to be single-valued on $Σ$. Regularity implies that the number of interior zeros of one differential equals the total rotation of the phase of the ratio of the two differentials along the boundary. It also bounds this rotation on each boundary component by the number of poles on that component. Combined with the known count of zeros on the double of $Σ$, these two statements force $Σ$ to be a disk. With mutually local seven-branes and orientifolds as interior punctures, the same conclusion follows under additional assumptions on the global structure of the differentials and on their behavior at the punctures. The disk then admits at most one orientifold puncture. On a disk without punctures, the bound applied between successive poles also recovers the known cyclic ordering of the five-brane charges along the boundary.

</details>


### [116] [Cohomological Foundations of Entropic Monotonicity: The Relative Cyclic Complex, the Transgression Map, and the Entropic $c$-Theorem in $d = 2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24396)
*R. C. Rashkov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于冯·诺依曼代数对的相对循环复形和忠实法态相关的横越映射的上同调框架，用于二维熵c定理；揭示了熵证明中正性本质源于相对模余圈零频分量的Borchers正性，并指出该正性具有维度无关性；主要结果包括纠缠上同调的刻画、相对模余圈的Borchers正性、熵流的有限维正性，以及无需紫外截断的二维熵c定理证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解熵c定理背后的数学结构与物理本质，特别是其中正性的算子代数起源（如量子零能量条件QNEC），需建立一个统一、严谨且维度无关的上同调框架。

Method: 基于冯·诺依曼代数对的相对循环复形与忠实法态诱导的横越映射，结合分裂性质、模p-核性及半侧模包含结构，发展出一套共调框架；利用Borchers正性、模余圈分析与一维分析技术推导关键结论。

Result: （i）纠缠上同调等于限制映射的核（定理2.6）；（ii）相对模余圈满足Borchers正性（命题3.8）；（iii）熵流具有有限维正性（推论3.9）；（iv）在d=2时严格证明熵c定理（定理4.3），全程无需紫外调节器。

Conclusion: 熵c定理的正性根源在于算子代数层面的Borchers正性，该框架不仅统一解释了二维结果，还具备向高维（如三维F定理）推广的潜力；所提两个猜想（横越映射上链正性与QNEC的上同调诠释）为后续研究指明方向，但不影响本文核心结论的自洽性。

Abstract: We develop a cohomological framework for the entropic $c$-theorem in two dimensions, based on the relative cyclic complex of a pair of von Neumann algebras and on the transgression map associated with a faithful normal state. The framework identifies the positivity underlying the entropic proof as Borchers positivity of the zero-frequency component of the relative modular cocycle, which is the operator-algebraic origin of the quantum null energy condition (QNEC). The positivity results are dimension-independent: they depend only on the split property, modular $p$-nuclearity, and the half-sided modular inclusion structure.
  The main results of the paper are: (i) the identification of the entanglement cohomology with the kernel of the restriction map (Theorem 2.6); (ii) Borchers positivity of the relative modular cocycle (Proposition 3.8}); (iii) the finite-dimensional positivity of the entropy flux (Corollary 3.9); and (iv) the entropic $c$-theorem in $d = 2$ (Theorem 4.3), which follows from (iii) and the one-variable analysis. The $c$-theorem proof uses no ultraviolet regulator at any intermediate step.
  We also formulate two open problems: the cochain-level positivity of the transgression (Conjecture 3.5) and the cohomological interpretation of the QNEC (Conjecture 3.11). Both are stated as research directions, not as results of the paper; in particular, the $c$-theorem proof does not depend on either of them.
  The extension of the framework to the $F$-theorem in $d = 3$ and the general-$d$ program is the subject of the companion paper \cite{Rashkov:2026b}.

</details>


### [117] [Localization of a 3-form gauge field in a braneworld](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24510)
*Pratik Chattopadhyay,M. J. Neves*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在5维AdS时空背景下，3-形式规范场在额外维度上的局域化问题，通过引入仅依赖于第五维坐标的标量场与3-形式场耦合模型，并利用分离变量法分析其局域化条件；进一步推广至复标量场与对偶场强张量的最小耦合，揭示了耦合常数对p-形式场在膜上局域化的重要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高维理论中高阶微分形式（特别是3-形式规范场）如何在弯曲额外维度（AdS几何）中实现局域化，以支持低能四维有效理论的物理现实性。

Method: 构建5维引力-标量-3-形式耦合模型，采用AdS度规并引入翘曲函数；对仅依赖第五维的标量场和3-形式场求解运动方程；运用变量分离法分析3-形式场的动力学模态；进一步推广为复标量场与3-形式场对偶强度张量的最小耦合。

Result: 得到了3-形式场在翘曲几何下局域化的条件，发现其零模能否局域化依赖于翘曲函数的具体形式；复标量场与对偶场强的耦合引入关键耦合常数，可调控新p-形式场在膜上的局域化行为。

Conclusion: 3-形式规范场可在AdS型翘曲额外维度中实现局域化，且其局域化性质受翘曲函数和标量-对偶场耦合强度共同决定；该机制为高维p-形式场在膜世界模型中的物理实现提供了新途径。

Abstract: The localization of a $3$-form gauge field is investigated in a extra dimension attached to an AdS metric in $4+1$ dimensions. We propose the study of a model for the scalar field and a $3$-form gauge field coupled to the gravitational field in $5D$. The spacetime is set by an AdS metric whose geometry introduces a warped function of the fifth spatial coordinate. Considering that the scalar field also depends only on the extra coordinate, we obtain the equation of motions for the set of fields of the model. Using the technique of variable separation in the $3$-rank gauge sector, we explore the localization of the dynamical in terms of the warped function. We also extend the initial model for a complex scalar field that couples minimally with the dual strength field tensor associated with the $3$-rank gauge field. Thus, the coupling constant has a fundamental role in the localization of new $p$-form gauge field on the brane domain.

</details>


### [118] [$1/N$ corrections to $\langle TJJ\rangle$ and $\langle TTT\rangle$ in QED$_3$, QED$_3$--GNY and scalar QED$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24531)
*Yongwei Guo,Zhijin Li,Ke Ren*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了三维QED理论中应力张量T与守恒流J的三点半相关函数⟨TJJ⟩和⟨TTT⟩在大味数展开下的首阶修正，涵盖QED₃、QED₃-Gross-Neveu-Yukawa及临界标量QED₃模型，并关联到共形对撞机物理与能流响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维阿贝尔规范理论在去禁闭量子临界性、狄拉克自旋液体和铜氧化物超导中具有广泛应用；而守恒流J与应力张量T的关联函数承载关键动力学信息，但强耦合下缺乏高精度解析结果。

Method: 采用大味数（large-N_f）展开方法，计算⟨TJJ⟩和⟨TTT⟩三点半函数的首阶亚主导修正；同时结合平直空间能量流分析（尤其在自由场主项为零的方向）与扭曲球面自由能导数两种独立路径进行交叉验证。

Result: 得到了QED₃类模型中⟨TJJ⟩（含全局味流与拓扑U(1)流）和⟨TTT⟩的首阶大N修正；明确了这些方向上能量流由四粒子产生主导；确定了所有独立张量结构的系数；并复现了共形对撞机参数的解析修正。

Conclusion: 该工作为强耦合三维规范理论中的输运性质（如电导率、剪切响应）和共形自举研究提供了关键基准数据，强化了大N方法在非微扰共形场论中的应用能力。

Abstract: Three-dimensional Abelian gauge theories have broad applications in deconfined quantum criticality, Dirac spin liquids and cuprate superconductivity. Correlation functions of conserved currents $J$ and the stress tensor $T$ carry fundamental information about these theories. We compute the first subleading large-flavor corrections to the parity-even three-point functions $\langle TJJ\rangle$ and $\langle TTT\rangle$ in QED$_3$, QED$_3$--Gross--Neveu--Yukawa, and critical scalar QED$_3$. For $\langle TJJ\rangle$, we study both global flavor currents and the topological $U(1)$ current, which measures the magnetic flux of the dynamical gauge field. We evaluate the flat-space energy flux in directions where the leading free-field profile vanishes. At first subleading order the flux in these directions is determined by four-particle production, yielding analytic corrections to the conformal collider parameters. We reproduce the corrections independently from squashed-sphere free-energy derivatives. Together with known two-point normalizations, the collider parameters fix the coefficients of both independent tensor structures in each correlator. Our results are useful for studying conductivity and shear response near quantum critical points and for conformal bootstrap studies of conserved-current and stress-tensor correlators in strongly coupled gauge theories.

</details>


### [119] [Deep learning spacetime from entanglement dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24666)
*Hyun-Sik Jeong,Keun-Young Kim,Gaya Yun,Kwan Yun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用神经常微分方程，从热场双态的纠缠熵S(t)数据中直接学习并重建反德西特（AdS）黑洞时空度规，发现S(t)仅依赖于以类时Killing范数表示的局域表面引力，足以唯一确定单函数未知度规（如带电BTZ黑洞），并复现霍金温度与可探测内部边界；但对含两个未知函数的度规，不同物理几何若在(t,z)二维截面上互为微分同胚，则给出相同S(t)，无法区分；网络亦自然满足因果约束v_E ≤ 1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子纠缠（特别是边界纠缠熵S(t)）能在多大程度上决定和重构体时空几何，尤其是黑洞内部结构，从而为ER=EPR猜想和时空涌现提供数据驱动验证。

Method: 采用神经常微分方程（Neural ODE）模型，直接从热场双态下两边界半直线子系统的纠缠熵S(t)时间演化数据中学习Bulk度规；结合几何分析，识别S(t)所依赖的关键几何量（局域表面引力），并检验不同度规自由度下的可重构性。

Result: 1）S(t)仅通过局域表面引力（以类时Killing范数表达）依赖于几何；2）对单未知函数度规（如带电BTZ），网络能准确重建包括部分黑洞内部的度规，并复现霍金温度与临界表面位置；3）对双未知函数度规，存在不可区分的微分同胚等价类；4）网络隐式满足因果约束v_E ≤ 1，不支持违反该约束的数据。

Conclusion: 纠缠熵S(t)足以重构具有特定对称性的简单黑洞度规之关键特征（如温度、内部可探边界），但不能完全确定一般度规的所有自由度；结果划定了仅凭纠缠所能确定的时空几何范围，支持‘时空从纠缠中涌现’的观点，同时明确了其内在局限性。

Abstract: In the eternal AdS black hole, the entanglement of the thermofield double state is geometrically realised by the Einstein--Rosen bridge, in line with the ER=EPR conjecture. Hartman--Maldacena surfaces threading this bridge pass behind the event horizon, so the time-dependent entanglement entropy $S(t)$ of a pair of half-lines, one on each boundary, probes the black hole interior. We turn this relation around and ask how much of the bulk spacetime can be reconstructed from $S(t)$, using a neural ordinary differential equation that learns the metric directly from the entanglement data. We show that $S(t)$ depends on the geometry only through a single function, the local surface gravity expressed in terms of the timelike Killing norm. This is enough to fix a metric with one unknown function: for charged BTZ black holes, the network reconstructs it throughout the region probed by the surfaces, including part of the interior, and the learned metric also yields the Hawking temperature and the location of the critical surface that bounds the accessible interior. With two unknown functions, however, it is not: physically distinct geometries whose two-dimensional $(t,z)$ sectors are related by diffeomorphisms produce the same $S(t)$. Finally, although the null energy condition is not imposed, the network reproduces data obeying the causal bound $v_E\le 1$ on the entanglement velocity, but not data that violate it. Our results thus offer a data-driven perspective on how spacetime, including the black hole interior, emerges from quantum entanglement, and delineate which features of the geometry entanglement alone can determine.

</details>


### [120] [Regge Dilaton Gravity from Feynman Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24691)
*Florian Girelli,Christopher Pollack*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将Baratin和Freidel关于三维和四维欧氏空间中闭合费曼图的Regge型拓扑模型表示，推广到二维情形（球面、平坦或双曲背景几何），证明任意闭合平面标量图的约化费曼振幅可表示为离散BF理论或线性稀释子引力Regge离散化模型中的可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Baratin和Freidel在三维和四维欧氏空间中建立的闭合费曼图与Regge型拓扑模型之间的联系，推广至二维具有不同曲率背景几何的情形。

Method: 通过构建适配于二维球面、平坦或双曲背景几何的离散BF理论（以对应等距群为规范群）及线性稀释子引力的齐次Regge离散化模型，将闭合平面标量图的约化费曼振幅表示为相应模型配分函数中的可观测量插入。

Result: 成功将任意闭合（平面）标量图的约化费曼振幅表示为两种离散引力模型（离散BF理论或线性稀释子引力的Regge离散化）配分函数中的可观测量。

Conclusion: 二维不同背景几何下，闭合费曼图仍可由Regge型拓扑/引力模型描述，表明此类模型具有更广泛的适用性，并为低维量子引力与量子场论的统一提供了新视角。

Abstract: Baratin and Freidel showed that closed Feynman diagrams in three and four Euclidean dimensions can be expressed as expectation values of observables in a Regge-type topological model \cite{baratin_hidden_2007, baratin_hidden_2007-1}. We extend their analysis to two dimensions with spherical, flat, or hyperbolic background geometry. Specifically, we show that the reduced Feynman amplitude of any closed (planar) scalar graph can be represented as an observable insertion in the partition function of either a discretized BF theory, whose gauge group is the isometry group of the background, or a homogeneous Regge discretization of linear dilaton gravity.

</details>


### [121] [Learning the Anyon Atlas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24752)
*Jonah Berean-Dutcher,Clay Cordova,Julio Parra-Martinez,Richard Tuddenham,Zekai Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文扩展了之前对2+1维拓扑量子场论模数据的搜索，将任意子数量从22提升至42，新发现150,679组模数据；通过引入基于逻辑回归的三个拒绝门优化算法，并利用该大规模数据集否证了Geiko-Moore猜想，提出更精确的时间反演对称性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展对2+1维拓扑量子场论模数据的系统性搜索，验证和修正关于时间反演对称性的理论猜想，并提升算法可扩展性以探索更高秩模数据。

Method: 在原有搜索算法基础上新增三个拒绝门（其中两个基于逻辑回归），用于筛除不太可能生成有效模数据的SL(2,Z)表示；结合已知构造闭包估计覆盖度，并开源全部结果。

Result: 发现150,679组新模数据（秩22→42）；证伪Geiko-Moore猜想并提出改进判据；首次在秩100找到非Chern-Simons实现的素模数据；建成AnyonAtlas.org数据库。

Conclusion: 模数据的系统性枚举可有效驱动理论物理猜想的检验与演进；带学习型拒绝门的算法显著提升高秩搜索效率与可靠性；开源数据平台为后续研究提供坚实基础。

Abstract: We extend our previous search for the modular data of topological quantum field theories in 2+1 dimensions from 22 to 42 anyons, identifying 150,679 new modular data. We do so by adding to our algorithm three rejection gates, two of them based on logistic regression, that discard representations of $SL(2,\mathbb{Z})$ unlikely to yield modular data. We estimate that roughly 80% of the modular data between ranks 23 and 42 has been identified after closing under known constructions. With this data, we find counterexamples to a conjecture of Geiko and Moore that the reality of the higher Gauss sums suffices for time-reversal symmetry in Chern-Simons theories, and propose a refined criterion. We demonstrate the scalability of this gated implementation by identifying prime modular data at rank 100 that is not realized by any Chern-Simons theory. The identified data through rank 42 has been made available at https://AnyonAtlas.org, to which further data will be added as it is found.

</details>


### [122] [Computing $F$-Symbols from Modular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24753)
*Jonah Berean-Dutcher,Clay Cordova,Julio Parra-Martinez,Richard Tuddenham*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从模数据出发计算具有自对偶单生成元的幺正模范畴F-符号的新策略，避免了直接求解复杂的五边形方程，通过逐步提升辫群表示维度来递推确定F-符号，并支持精确恢复与严格验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖非线性求解五边形方程，计算困难，尤其对高秩、非无重（non-multiplicity-free）或大融合系数的模范畴效率低下；需发展更鲁棒、可扩展且能保证精度的F-符号计算策略。

Method: 基于自对偶单生成元的模范畴结构，构建逐级增加辫子数的辫群表示序列，从中提取约束以逐步确定F-符号；结合数值实现与代数数域假设，利用LLL算法等恢复精确表达式，并对所有五边形和六边形方程进行精确验证。

Result: 成功计算了秩11–12多个困难案例的F-符号，包括Z(Haagerup₃)及奇异各向异性模范畴B11-00119（最大融合系数为4），在普通笔记本上耗时约10分钟；所有结果均可精确恢复并严格验证。

Conclusion: 该策略显著降低了F-符号计算的复杂性与对范畴结构的限制（如无需无重性），兼具数值效率与代数严谨性，为模范畴分类与任意子物理建模提供了实用新工具。

Abstract: We present a strategy for computing the $F$-symbols of unitary modular categories with a self-dual simple generator, starting from their modular data, which almost entirely avoids the non-linear solution of the pentagon equations. The algorithm climbs a ladder of braid group representations on successively more strands of the generator until enough $F$-symbols have been determined to propagate them to a complete solution. The strategy presented is successful in difficult cases through ranks 11 and 12, including $\operatorname{Z}(\mathrm{Haagerup}_3)$, and does not require the category to be multiplicity-free. Although the algorithm is implemented numerically, we describe a reliable means of recovering the exact $F$-symbols, assuming that in some gauge they lie in a given number field, and of certifying every pentagon and hexagon equation exactly. The exotic rank-11 anisotropic modular category B11-00119 (https://anyonatlas.org/data/B11-00119), with largest fusion coefficient 4, is used as a motivating example, for which the solve takes roughly 10 minutes on a laptop. We will continue to add $F$-symbol solutions to https://AnyonAtlas.org as they are completed.

</details>


### [123] [Holography at finite resolution: zero-point length in AdS/CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24779)
*Ankit Anand,Kimet Jusufi,Amir A. Khodahami,Adrinil Paul,Ahmad Sheykhi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了T对偶启发的有效场论中零点长度l₀在全息理论中的两种不同解释：作为边界分辨尺度和作为AdS不变的体空间固有长度，并分别分析了其在边界响应、纠缠熵、互信息及传播子性质等方面的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索T对偶启发的有效场论中零点长度l₀在全息原理下的物理意义与作用，统一理解其在紫外有限性、纠缠结构与量子传播子中的双重角色。

Method: 将l₀分别解释为边界分辨尺度（滤波源项）和AdS不变的体空间固有长度；结合AdS/CFT对应、标量场bulk-to-boundary传播子、Ryu-Takayanagi公式、互信息计算及Källén-Lehmann谱分解等方法进行分析。

Result: 1) 作为边界分辨尺度时，l₀给出紫外有限且具幂律尾部的边界响应；确保纠缠熵满足周长律与球面自由能普适性；解析给出AdS₃中两区间互信息的连通性相变；2) 作为体空间固有长度时，l₀使单圈蝌蚪图有限并保持边界两点函数共形形式，但其Källén-Lehmann谱出现负权重，无法对应幺正理论；L/l₀表征形状因子的交叉尺度而非幺正补全的能谱。

Conclusion: 零点长度l₀在全息框架下具有双重几何/物理诠释，其作为边界分辨尺度更具一致性与可观测性；而作为体空间固有长度虽改善紫外发散，却牺牲幺正性，暗示其更宜视为有效尺度而非基本长度。

Abstract: We explore the zero-point length $l_0$ of T-duality-inspired effective field theory in holography, distinguishing two readings. As a boundary resolution scale, $l_0$ filters the source of a bulk scalar in anti-de Sitter space and yields an exactly solvable boundary response that is ultraviolet finite without a hard cutoff and inherits the power-law tails of the zero-point-length kernel. We show that this filter is the source-normalized bulk-to-boundary transfer function of a $ν=1$ scalar evaluated at radial depth $z=l_0$. Anchoring Ryu-Takayanagi surfaces on the same radial surface gives finite entanglement entropies that retain the perimeter law and the universal sphere free energy. For two intervals in AdS$_3$, the prescription yields the mutual-information connectivity transition in closed form for arbitrary interval lengths; equal intervals maximize the connectivity window at fixed total length, and in the subresolution regime the critical separation exhibits a universal scaling law with a rescaled mutual information collapsing onto a piecewise-linear profile. As an AdS-invariant bulk proper length, $l_0$ renders the one-loop tadpole finite while preserving the conformal form of boundary two-point functions. Its exact Källén-Lehmann decomposition, however, has negative continuum weight beginning at threshold, obstructing a unitary interpretation of this propagator. The ratio $L/l_0$ identifies a crossover scale of the form factor rather than a derived spectrum of a unitary completion.

</details>


### [124] [Rotating black hole thermodynamics at NNLO in higher-curvature gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24874)
*Cristobal Laporte*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了拟拓扑引力中高曲率修正对黑洞热力学的影响，利用微扰欧几里得作用量方法，精确计算了五维渐近平直、等角动量旋转黑洞的吉布斯自由能至二阶修正，并完整保留自旋参数依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高曲率引力修正（如Gauss-Bonnet和三次拟拓扑项）如何影响黑洞热力学量，特别是对旋转黑洞的非慢转情形下保持精确解析结构的需求。

Method: 采用微扰欧几里得作用量方法，在静态球对称黑洞基准上展开；针对五维等角动量旋转黑洞，对爱因斯坦-高斯-博内-三次拟拓扑引力模型，计算吉布斯自由能至下一阶以上（NNLO），保留全部自旋参数依赖与任意耦合系数。

Result: 得到了含二次与三次曲率修正的吉布斯自由能、质量、熵与角动量的解析表达式；揭示了旋转显著影响修正项的大小与符号。

Conclusion: 高曲率修正对旋转黑洞热力学的影响不可忽略，且其效应具有强自旋依赖性；该方法可推广至任意耦合强度，为高阶引力理论中的黑洞物理提供系统分析框架。

Abstract: We investigate higher-curvature corrections to black-hole thermodynamics in quasitopological gravity using perturbative Euclidean-action methods. Static, spherically symmetric black holes provide a benchmark for the action expansion, while our main application concerns five-dimensional, asymptotically flat, rotating black holes with equal angular momenta. For Einstein gravity supplemented by Gauss--Bonnet and cubic quasitopological interactions, we compute the Gibbs free energy through next-to-next-to-leading order, retaining both quadratic and cubic corrections. The result retains the full dependence on the background spin parameter, without a slow-rotation expansion, and extends to arbitrary couplings of quadratic and cubic curvature invariants. We derive the corrected mass, entropy and angular momenta, and show how rotation affects the size and sign of the corrections.

</details>


### [125] [Beyond hidden zeros and uniqueness in Tr$(φ^3)$: Block and rod zeros in any Tr$(φ^m)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24897)
*Laurentiu Rodina*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文扩展了隐藏零点的概念到任意Tr(φ^m)理论，提出了适用于奇数m的块零点和适用于所有m≥4的杆零点，并证明杆零点可唯一确定树图振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将隐藏零点这一在Tr(φ³)理论中有效的振幅重构工具推广至更一般的Tr(φ^m)理论，探索其普适性与物理意义。

Method: 通过构造正交动量和定义块零点（适用于奇数m），并引入基于杆堆积问题的非线性杆零点（适用于所有m≥4），结合矩阵秩条件与阈值消零现象进行分析。

Result: 证明杆零点在局域性假设下可唯一确定所有m≥4的Tr(φ^m)树图振幅；提出块零点可能同时约束局域性与幺正性的猜想。

Conclusion: 隐藏零点结构具有广泛适用性，块零点与杆零点分别揭示了不同m值下振幅的深层对称性与约束机制，为振幅重构与基本原理研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: Hidden zeros have proven powerful tools for reconstructing amplitudes in Tr$(φ^3)$, a theory of colored scalars with cubic interactions. We show that these structures extend to any Tr$(φ^m)$ theory. For odd $m$, we construct \emph{block zeros} from orthogonal sums of consecutive momenta, generalizing the original cubic hidden zeros. For every $m\geq4$, including even $m$, we describe a second family of nonlinear \emph{rod zeros} through a rod packing problem. In Tr$(φ^4)$ this construction reduces to a simple rank-one condition on a matrix of momentum contractions. We connect this new zero to the classic threshold nullification zeros of Voloshin, where certain amplitudes in massive $φ^4$ theory vanish when all produced particles are at rest. Finally, we demonstrate the reconstruction power of these zeros. Assuming locality, we prove that the rod zeros uniquely fix all Tr$(φ^m)$ tree amplitudes for $m\geq4$ up to normalization. We also conjecture that the block zeros enforce both locality and unitarity for any Tr$(φ^m)$ theory with odd $m$.

</details>
